(2) **TTL + expiry**: a lease carries `expires_at_utc`; an expired lease is reclaimable (a crashed holder never deadlocks the resource); `renew` extends it. (3) **Race-safe stale reclaim**: a per-stale-lease atomic CLAIM marker serializes reclaim + a RE-VERIFY-before-replace + an atomic `File.Move` overwrite (no delete+create gap) -- exactly one reclaimer ever takes a given stale lease. (4) **Holder identity** (`$env:LIFEORCH_INSTANCE` or `<host>:<pid>:<guid8>`) + a returned **lease_id** token; release/renew require it (or a matching holder), so a slow holder can never release/renew a lease already reclaimed from it; **same-holder re-attach** for crash-recovery. (5) **Blocking or non-blocking** acquire (`-WaitSeconds`). Deterministic (confidence:null, no provenance), **`parallel_safe:true`** (designed for concurrent invocation), **NOT a review-queue producer**. Pure PowerShell + .NET -- no external binary / Python / model / `models.json` change / Module 7 re-verify.
- **conventional resources:** `gpu` (one `llama-server`/diffusers pipeline at a time -- every model module is `parallel_safe:false`), `git` (the commit lock -- index.lock collisions bit in D-0048/D-0049), `doc:<path>` (doc-ownership for a shared core-doc edit). Any string is a valid resource. **Acquire-order rule** (deadlock avoidance across multiple held resources): `gpu` before `git` before `doc:*`, release in reverse.
- **the caught bug (why the LIVE gate matters):** the cloud gate (38/38, incl. N-way cross-process mutual-exclusion + concurrent stale-reclaim) was green, but the FIRST live run FAILED fail-closed (no commit) on a **UTC-7 timezone bug**: `ConvertFrom-Json` hands back a Kind=Utc `[DateTime]` for a `...Z` field, and the code re-stringified it (culture-formatted, Kind dropped) then re-parsed with `ToUniversalTime()`, which on a non-UTC box shifted expiry by the local offset (-7h) -- so leases never looked expired and stale-reclaim produced ZERO winners. Reproduced locally by forcing `TZ=America/Los_Angeles`; fixed with a Kind-aware `ConvertTo-Utc` that uses the JSON `[DateTime]` directly. A second Windows-only issue (delete+create hitting NTFS pending-delete) was pre-empted by the atomic `File.Move` overwrite + `FileShare.Delete` on readers. Re-validated 38/38 under both `TZ=UTC` and `TZ=America/Los_Angeles`.
- **reason:** the multi-instance direction (D-0050) and the fan-out orchestrator (D-0051) both require a coordination layer before N Claude instances can drive the box safely; a filesystem lease with a TTL is the smallest primitive that covers the GPU lease + the git/commit lock + doc-ownership at once, and it matches the project coordination doctrine (D-0003: atomic filesystem ops on one volume, no network listener).
- **alternatives:** an OS file-handle lock held for the process lifetime (rejected -- a lease must span a separate model call across acquire/release invocations and survive a crash via TTL, not a handle); a blind rename-aside reclaim (rejected -- it let a straggler destroy the winner's fresh lease -> multiple winners; the claim-marker + re-verify is correct, proven by the concurrent-reclaim test); an executor-daemon lease concept (deferred -- a standalone filesystem primitive is simpler and works for cloud instances that never touch the executor); a fair FIFO/priority queue (deferred -- MVP is TTL + retry, not ordered fairness); wiring consumers now (deferred -- touches central `parallel_safe:false` modules; per-consumer follow-on).
- **tests / ship:** off-machine **38/38** on cloud pwsh 7.4.6 (real skill; S9 N-way concurrency = exactly one winner, S9b concurrent stale-reclaim = exactly one reclaimer, TTL expiry, blocking acquire, re-attach, error paths) -> shipped 8 files byte-exact via the job-runner -> **41/41 `-Live`** in one `dev.ship` call (`m29-reslease-ship-003`, commit **36d7e0be**): sha 8/8 + AST 2/2 + the real harness on the box (real cross-process concurrency + the Module 1 wrapper) + **0 orphaned llama-server/python**; committed exactly the 8 files with trailers. The first live run correctly FAILED fail-closed on the timezone bug (no commit) before the fix.
- **consequences:** the multi-instance buildout + the fan-out orchestrator now have their coordination primitive. New files under `modules/29-resource-lease/`. **No `models.json` / no `TOOL_MODEL_REGISTRY` model / no `REVIEW_QUEUE` producer change** (a deterministic tool; the ten-producer set is untouched). -- **affects:** Module 29 (new), `CURRENT_STATE.md`, `MODULE_ROADMAP.md`, `TOOL_MODEL_REGISTRY.md`, `REVIEW_QUEUE.md`, `HANDOFF.md`, `START_HERE.md`; realizes D-0050/D-0051 (the lock/lease prerequisite). -- **revisit-if:** the `gpu`/`git`/`doc:*` leases are wired into consumers; the fan-out orchestrator is built on top; a fair FIFO/priority queue, reader-writer locks, a warm-server lease, or an auto-renew helper is needed.

### D-0054 -- orchestrate.fanout (Module 30): the fan-out orchestrator, built on res.lease #29; SHIPPED via the job-runner
- **date:** 2026-07-27 -- **state:** locked (the module + the FANOUT_PROTOCOL operating doc + the compose-res.lease / emit-verification-packet design), provisional (a fan-out dogfood + consumer wiring + follow-ons)
- **decision:** Built + shipped **Module 30 `orchestrate.fanout`** (`modules/30-orchestrate-fanout/`), the fan-out orchestrator D-0051 named, built ON TOP OF `res.lease` #29 (D-0053). A conforming deterministic skill -- one action per invocation (`plan | report | status | handoff | list`) -- that is the mechanical scaffolding a Claude orchestrator instance drives to run N parallel worker Cowork sessions safely. It CANNOT (and must not) spawn or drive AI sessions -- the workers are HUMAN-dispatched (the D-0051/D-0052 boundary); it produces + manages every deterministic artifact around them.
  - **plan** turns worker unit-specs into a collision-safe plan: per worker the ordered lease set (acquire-order **gpu -> git -> doc:<path>**, release reverse) + the exact `res.lease` acquire/release command lines embedded in that worker's prompt; `dispatch_now` (trial of `-MaxParallel`=2, **clamped to at most ONE gpu worker**) vs. `queued`; `conflicts` (gpu serialization + doc-ownership contention); one prompt per worker + exactly one Nicholas check-in prompt; an optional read-only `res.lease list` **preflight** (composes #29, skips cleanly if unresolvable) that warns on a live conflict. Persists the plan under a shared plans dir.
  - **report** records a worker's `{state, summary, needs}` as ONE FILE PER REPORT (so N workers report concurrently with no contention -> parallel_safe). **status** rolls it up + computes `ready_for_handoff` per the cadence (`on_all` = every worker terminal; `on_each` = >=1 terminal). **handoff** assembles a summary + next-iteration prompts + one check-in + a **`lifeorch.verification.packet/0.1`** (one `run_module`/`human_action` item per worker output) -- realizing "the Verification Console's packet/result is the orchestrator's human-I/O" (D-0051).
  - Deterministic (confidence:null, empty provenance), **`parallel_safe:true`**, orchestrator/**NON-producer** (no review-queue writes; the ten-producer set untouched). Pure PowerShell + .NET -- **no external binary / Python / model / `models.json` change / Module 7 re-verify.** Ships a **`FANOUT_PROTOCOL.md`** operating-protocol doc (how an orchestrator instance drives it: the loop, resource discipline gpu->git->doc, trial-of-2 / clamp-to-1, the human-courier boundary, the orchestrator-owns-shared-docs rule).
- **tests / ship:** cloud pre-ship **51/51** on pwsh 7.4.6 (plan schedule + GPU clamp + doc-ownership conflict + the acquire-order + the embedded res.lease commands; the res.lease preflight seam via a mock res.lease; report/status/ready-cadence on_all+on_each; handoff -> a valid verification packet + next prompts + one check-in; list; error paths; deterministic; non-producer; the Module 1 wrapper) -> shipped 10 files byte-exact via the job-runner -> **committed `2ffe162e`** in ONE `dev.ship` call (`m30-fanout-ship-001`): sha 10/10 + AST 3/3 + the **51/51 harness live on the box** (incl. the real Module 1 wrapper) + **0 orphaned llama-server/python**; committed exactly the 10 files with trailers.
- **consequences:** the MULTI-INSTANCE buildout now has BOTH primitives -- the coordination lease (#29) and the orchestration scaffolding (#30). The "one active unit per session" rule (D-0029) can now relax to "one unit per instance, coordinated by leases + driven by a fan-out plan." New files under `modules/30-orchestrate-fanout/`. **No `models.json` / no `TOOL_MODEL_REGISTRY` model / no `REVIEW_QUEUE` producer change** (a deterministic tool). -- **affects:** Module 30 (new), `CURRENT_STATE.md`, `MODULE_ROADMAP.md`, `TOOL_MODEL_REGISTRY.md`, `HANDOFF.md`, `START_HERE.md`; realizes D-0050/D-0051 (the fan-out orchestrator). -- **revisit-if:** a **fan-out DOGFOOD** (Claude writes a real 2-worker plan, Nicholas runs it end-to-end) validates the loop + surfaces UX gaps; res.lease consumers are wired (`gpu`/`git`/`doc:*` into the model modules + dev.ship); Governor Phase 2 (warm server) is wanted; a fair/priority scheduler, live worker monitoring, lease auto-renew, or per-worker tool-scoping is needed.

### D-0055 -- Fan-out DOGFOOD (iteration 1): orchestrate.fanout #30 driven end-to-end; 3 parallel workers shipped (res.lease gpu+git consumers wired; frontier.bridge #31 built)
- **date:** 2026-07-27 -- **state:** locked (the fan-out loop works end-to-end; the three units shipped + committed), provisional (iteration-2 units + the Verification Console audit of the emitted packet)
- **context:** the D-0054 handoff named a fan-out DOGFOOD as the next unit -- write a real multi-worker plan and have Nicholas run it end-to-end, to validate the multi-instance loop (orchestrate.fanout #30 on res.lease #29) and surface UX gaps. The user asked to add a THIRD, non-interfering worker that builds the manual frontier bridge (frontier.bridge, D-0052) alongside the two natural dogfood workers.
- **decision / what ran:** the orchestrator instance drove the REAL `orchestrate.fanout -Action plan` (not a hand-authored plan) with 3 worker specs -> plan `fo-1-20ed8a0b` (MaxParallel 2, report_back on_all): A-gpu-lease (GPU), B-git-lease (CPU), C-frontier-bridge (CPU, NEW module). The plan reported dispatch_now=[A,B], queued=[C], **0 gpu-serialized + 0 doc-contention conflicts**, preflight clean. Nicholas dispatched each worker prompt into a fresh Cowork session; each acquired its res.lease(s) in gpu->git->doc order, did ONE scoped unit, shipped via dev.ship, and reported done. `-Action status` = 3/3 done, ready_for_handoff true; `-Action handoff` emitted a `lifeorch.verification.packet/0.1` (3 items) + iteration-2 prompts + one check-in.
- **units shipped (all via dev.ship, under the res.lease git lease):**
  - **A -- model.gateway #7 gpu consumer** (`0c6d5c9`): acquire the res.lease `gpu` lease before starting llama-server, release by lease_id after teardown; graceful `off|auto|wait|require` (log+proceed if contended/absent). Module 7 tests 42/42 (also fixed a pre-existing stale wired-LLM count assertion 4->5, the D-0044 9B tier); a live gen confirmed the lease held across the run and released after.

### D-0056 -- Fan-out iterations 2-3: doc:<path> consumer + Module 30 fix shipped; E (warm-server) failed + WEDGED the executor; executor+watchdog HARDENED
- **date:** 2026-07-28 -- **state:** locked (iter 2 D+F shipped; the wedge incident + recovery; iter 3 hardening shipped + restarted-live), provisional (E rerun in iteration 4; the accumulated verification packets not yet run in the Console)
- **iteration 2 (plan fo-2-b991c65f, 3 workers D/E/F):**
  - **D -- doc:<path> res.lease consumer -> doc.io #20** (`d2a7352`, 106/106 = 88 baseline + 18 lease): opt-in `-Lease` acquires the `doc:<relpath>` lease around the read-modify-write and releases by lease_id; graceful when off / res.lease absent; `doc_lease_unavailable` when contended. **This COMPLETES the res.lease consumer trio: gpu->model.gateway #7 (D-0055/A), git->dev.ship (D-0055/B), doc->doc.io (here).**
  - **F -- Module 30 prompt-template fix** (`581f854`, 56/56): restored "Acquire returns a lease_id" (a backtick-escape had eaten the leading A) and dropped the dangling "GPU clamp below" check-in reference; added tests. Closes the two D-0055 cosmetic findings; verified live (iteration-3 prompts render correctly).
  - **E -- Governor Phase 2 warm server (#7): FAILED.** The probe launched a warm/persistent llama-server inside a task that then BLOCKED; the task exceeded its 300 s timeout, and the executor's timeout-kill could not reap the detached llama-server (.NET Kill(true) misses a grandchild), so the orphaned server kept a handle on a file in running/ and the executor poll loop LIVELOCKED retrying that finalize-move every ~3 s while never claiming pending. The heartbeat stayed fresh, so the watchdog (session-scoped; also not running) was blind. A worker mis-read this as "no executor."
- **the wedge incident + recovery:** the executor was up but wedged ~00:29-01:42Z. It could NOT run its own queued cleanup (E-warm-cleanup-1, a taskkill llama-server task) because the wedge stopped it claiming pending. Recovered OUT-OF-BAND: killed the orphaned llama-server.exe (PID 37704, ~340 MB) via Task Manager over the computer-use bridge (device_bash is Linux; terminals are grant-restricted to click-only, so Task Manager End task was the path). The instant the lock released, the executor finalized the stuck probe as failed, auto-ran E-warm-cleanup-1, and returned to idle/healthy. Lesson: a persistent/detached server must be launched so the task RETURNS (server outlives the job); a task that blocks holding the server both fails on timeout and orphans the server.
- **iteration 3 (plan fo-3-cf4965fb, single worker G): executor+watchdog HARDENING SHIPPED** (`e5b93ab`; Module 0 16/16 + Module 00.1 33/33, verified under UTC + America/New_York). Deliberately a SINGLE isolated worker (core infra everything depends on). Fixes: (1) on timeout/cancel, reap the WHOLE process tree incl. a detached llama-server (per-PID kill of descendants, then root, then an orphan-name sweep) BEFORE the finalize move; (2) per-task finalize isolated + bounded-retry -- a blocked running->done move is DEFERRED and surfaces a `degraded` marker instead of starving the pending-claim step (one stuck task no longer wedges the queue); (3) heartbeat.json now carries health (`degraded`/`poll_error_streak`/`stuck_finalize_count`/`oldest_stuck_finalize_age_seconds`) and Get-WatchdogDecision + Get-ExecutorState detect a still-heartbeating-but-WEDGED executor (degraded, poll-error streak, OR a running task past timeout+grace) and kill_restart it. Also FIXED a timezone-dependent wedge-parse bug in Get-ExecutorState that made a real wedge invisible on the non-UTC Windows box (why the watchdog could never have caught this). Nicholas RESTARTED the executor onto the hardened code (new instance 30aa2f29, pid 32952, health fields live) and (re)started the watchdog.
- **findings status (from D-0055):** #1 (E warm-server needs a detached-server model) -> iteration 4 rerun; **#2 (executor hardening) RESOLVED (G)**; **#3 (watchdog blind to a heartbeating-but-wedged executor) RESOLVED (G)**.
- **affects:** res.lease #29 consumer trio complete; Module 0 + Module 00.1 hardened + restarted-live; Governor Phase 2 still OPEN (E rerun). -- **revisit-if:** iteration 4 (E detached warm server + 2 companions); run the accumulated verification packets (fo-1/fo-2/fo-3) through the Verification Console; Governor Phase 3 (auto-ramp) once Phase 2 lands.

The returned design REFINES the ADAPTIVE_RESOURCE_GOVERNOR section-4 sketch: make Phase 3 a MONOTONIC, MODEL-AFFINE EPOCH controller, NOT another answer-judging ladder -- M0 (3B floor) -> M1 (expanded-mid: one clean FRESH-CONTEXT retry, NO reload) -> S0 (9B strong, DIRECT one-rung decisions, not judge-over-mid) -> optional X0 (one-shot 27B emergency), NEVER de-escalating within a task. It flags that the current `max` profile (mid decisions + strong GENERATION) is NOT model-affine and can thrash mid<->strong reloads on the one-model GPU, so each epoch must run every LLM call on ONE resident model. The loop is closed by a PRE-FROZEN deterministic success contract (schema lifeorch.goal_verification/0.1; predicate vocabulary file_exists / sha256_equals_source / json_schema_valid / command_exit_zero / artifact_nonempty / state_version_changed / ...), a WHOLE-TASK gpu lease (acquire once, renew ~30s), and an EXACT resident-model manifest (model_sha256 + engine_build + context + gpu_layers + no_think) so a stale warm server can't be silently reused. Escalation = hard-failure triggers (empty/malformed/out-of-set/length-truncated/finish-but-verifier-fails/repeat-no-progress) -> immediate M0->S0, plus a 2-strike soft-signal accumulator; bounded by monotonic movement + max-1-swap + leave-resident-after (90s inter-task reuse window) + idempotency-keyed tools + resume-from-verified-state (no duplicate side effects after escalation). Smallest first slice (Stage 1): warm server + whole-task lease + M0 + one expanded-mid retry + one S0 escalation + hard/2-strike triggers + a caller-supplied success contract + a governor trace (EXCLUDE logprobs/entropy/27B/self-consistency/pattern-learning), preceded by a small live mid-vs-strong direct-decision calibration. Captured into claude/ADAPTIVE_RESOURCE_GOVERNOR.md (section 4 + the section-6 open items).
- **the fan-out loop, validated again:** iteration 5 ran the full plan -> dispatch -> workers -> report -> status -> handoff cycle cleanly; the handoff packet's NEW input-validation (MK's own shipped code, exercised LIVE on the handoff that emitted it) passed MK's {action:list} through clean and rendered TD as a human_action item.

### D-0061 -- Fan-out iteration 8: -AutoRamp default-on TRIED then REVERTED (contract-less regression caught live) + the 27B/X0 quant NEGATIVE RESULT (no fit) -> the resident 9B is the top rung; couriered frontier report recommends 9B Q5_K_M
- **date:** 2026-07-28 -- **state:** locked (iter 8 2/2 terminal; A shipped-then-reverted, B negative-result-by-design)
- **iteration 8 (plan fo-8-e6f2d44f, 2 workers, MaxParallel 2, 0 gpu/doc conflicts, 2/2 done).** Nicholas's "adopt + make X0 practical" choice: one CPU adoption unit + one GPU practicality probe, disjoint file sets.
  - **AR8-default-on (CPU) -- -AutoRamp default-on SHIPPED (`278c088`) then REVERTED (`fbacf69`).** The worker flipped agent.local's default to the -AutoRamp controller (opt-out preserved; Console toggle default-checked) and its MOCK gates passed (agent.local 102/102, AutoRamp 105, Console 89/89). BUT the orchestrator's real-model floor-check (the deferred live item) caught a regression: contract-less, the -AutoRamp controller has NO closing signal (it was designed to close on a frozen success contract), so it IGNORES the agent's own `finish` and loops to `max_steps`, returning `status=error` with `result=null`. Live: a "create a file" goal ran 8 decide steps + errored though the file was written correctly (the 3B chose finish at step 3 -- "goal already satisfied" -- and again at 4/5/6, all discarded); the strict-floor opt-out (-AutoRamp:$false) finished in 1 step, status completed. => the default-on flip REGRESSED every simple contract-less goal (8x the model calls + an error envelope). Per Nicholas, REVERTED 278c088 in full (`fbacf69`, verified byte-exact vs b1f36f0) -> the known-good opt-in default is restored; the -AutoRamp controller + flag + success-contract path remain opt-in (the full-commit revert also dropped the -NoAutoRamp alias 278c088 added; -AutoRamp:$false is the opt-out). The mock harness missed this because the mock model's finish was honored -- a real-model-only failure mode (the D-0032 "weak tiers under-use finish" behavior meets a controller that does not apply the D-0046 terminator without a contract).
  - **X0-quant (GPU) -- NEGATIVE RESULT, nothing shipped** (models.json + modules/21 + widgets/01 + core-docs UNTOUCHED): NO Qwen3.5-27B quant fits GPU-bound on the 11 GB RTX 2080 Ti (~9.9 GB free; IQ2_XXS 9.61 GB collapses quality; IQ3_XXS 12.8 / Q2_K 12.1 / Q3_K_M 14.8 GB over). Incumbent 27B Q4 decides correctly but 2.1 tok/s partial-offload (~88s warm/~170s cold). ### D-0062 — Fan-out iteration 9: contract-less -AutoRamp closing FIXED + default-on live; 9B strong tier -> Q5_K_M
- **date:** 2026-07-28 · **state:** locked
- **decision:** Fixed the D-0061 contract-less closing defect in agent.local #21 and re-enabled `-AutoRamp` DEFAULT-ON. Root cause was an inversion: the contract-less close required EVERY routed planned tool to succeed, which forced extra tools (fs.manage/gen.image) and looped to max_steps + status=error. The M0 epoch now closes a contract-less goal via the D-0046 deterministic terminator -- DONE once >=1 required side-effecting tool succeeds. `-NoAutoRamp` / `-AutoRamp:$false` reproduce the strict floor byte-for-byte. Separately upgraded the resident 9B strong tier Q4_K_M -> Q5_K_M (fidelity + KV headroom, per the couriered frontier report).
- **reason:** iteration 8 (D-0061) shipped default-on then reverted because a real-model floor-check caught the contract-less loop; the mock harness had missed it. The fix restores the fast-path (contract-less simple goal resolves at M0 in 1 substantive step, 0 escalation, 0 swaps) so default-on is safe. Q5_K_M is the report-recommended low-risk fidelity upgrade (binding budget is KV/context, not weight-fit).
- **alternatives:** keep -AutoRamp opt-in (rejected -- the governor directive wants ramp-toward-capacity by default); keep the 9B at Q4_K_M (rejected -- Q5_K_M fits with headroom and improves fidelity); chase a 27B rung (rejected D-0061 -- no quant fits GPU-bound on 11 GB).
- **consequences:** agent.local defaults to the -AutoRamp controller; contract-less goals cost exactly the strict floor; a supplied contract still ramps M0->M1->S0 on failure. Strong (S0) tier now loads Q5_K_M (~7.11 GB, live S0 6/6 @2048 tok, non-empty, finish=stop); the Q4 entry is retained as `llm.strong.qwen3p5-9b-q4` (wired:false) for a one-flip rollback. NOTE: the 32-token latency-calibration harness returns EMPTY on BOTH Q4 and Q5 (finish=length) -- documented behavior, not a regression; the 9B needs >=~1024 tokens to emit a decision, which is why S0 uses 2048.
- **verification:** fan-out plan fo-9-d4139304 (2 workers, 0 conflicts, 2/2). AR9-close e444851: real-model floor-check PASS (status=completed, accepted_epoch=M0, model_swaps=0, ar9ok.txt=ok, 0 orphans); off-machine AutoRamp 122/122 (incl. a new multi-side-effecting-tool regression reproducing the loop in the mock), agent.local 102/102, Console 89/89, route.tools 33/33. Q5-stage 0bd2733: Q5_K_M staged on F: (7,111,487,520 bytes, sha a686d88e, curl VERIFY=PASS), models.json repointed in place (11/11 GPU-free registry+gateway verify); orchestrator live S0 6/6 calibration @2048 tok PASS.
- **affects:** modules/21-agent-local (Invoke-AutoRamp.ps1, Invoke-AgentLocal.ps1), widgets/01-local-agent-console, modules/07-model-gateway/models.json (+ F: staged Q5_K_M GGUF).
- **revisit-if:** the warm multi-model pool + router lands (would generalize strong-tier residency); audio/image/video model leads arrive (generator modules #22-#25); a contract-less multi-tool goal is observed not closing at M0.

### D-0063 -- Fan-out iteration 10: warm multi-model pool + router, design-first (probe + design + frontier second opinion)
- **date:** 2026-07-29 - **state:** locked (iter 10 1/1 shipped; design-first)
- **decision:** Ran iteration 10 (plan fo-10-fbfbae02, 1 GPU worker WMP-design, 0 conflicts) as a DESIGN-FIRST unit for the warm multi-model pool + router (evolving model.gateway #7 Phase 2). Shipped modules/07-model-gateway/WARM_POOL_DESIGN.md (commit c07125f) grounded in a real-box probe (m10-warmpool-probe-002; 0 orphans, gpu lease clean), plus a frontier.bridge escalation pack (12da1fca) that Nicholas couriered to ChatGPT Pro; the returned second opinion is folded into the design (section 9).
- **measured (probe):** ONE ~7 GB model fits the 11 GB GPU at a time (9B Q5 resident -> 2902 MiB free; ~93k-token KV headroom @32 KiB/tok f16; a second ~7 GB model co-resident = False). Model swap is GPU-upload-bound (~1.6 s->3B / ~4.1 s->9B), same-model warm reuse ~1 ms, OS page-cache warmth does NOT cut swap cost. BOTH engine builds (b8661 + b10092) expose native multi-model flags (--models/--alias) + --slot-save-path + --cache-reuse + KV-quant (--cache-type-k/v); ABSENT --model-alias/--prompt-cache. All GGUFs (28.5 GiB) fit the 64 GB RAM.
- **mechanism decision:** Stage-1 = mechanism C -- extend the D-0057 detached warm server into a NAMED POOL MANAGER (Ensure-ResidentModel) + residency-key + task-affinity/model-affine-epoch swap-minimising policy + whole-task gpu lease + 90 s keep-resident window. Native router (mechanism A, --models) = GATED Stage-2+ (does not remove the GPU upload; --models-max is not a VRAM oracle -> OOM risk; needs a direct b8661/b10092 probe). Process-per-model (B) rejected (cannot keep weights GPU-resident on 11 GB). RAM-warm role-specialists do NOT earn their keep by warmth alone; only a coding specialist is a near-term candidate behind a >=30-task benchmark. KV: 16K f16 default; 32K conservative q8/q8 + flash-attn only if margin short, re-gated by S0 6/6 + long-context + stability. Engine: test b10092 as the universal build across all 5 fixtures (VLM = gating test); keep per-model engine_path if any regress.
- **reason:** the couriered frontier report (D-0061) recommended a warm multi-model pool; design-first (probe -> design -> frontier second opinion) avoids committing the build before the constraints + a second opinion are in, matching the Governor Phase 3 flow (D-0058/59).
- **verification:** commit c07125f scope-clean (1 file, ONLY WARM_POOL_DESIGN.md, 296 lines; no core-docs / models.json / other modules); probe 0 orphans + gpu lease released; worker report + findings.json/.md on disk; the ChatGPT Pro second opinion (18.6 KB) captured at frontier-pack-warmpool.answer.md CONFIRMS mechanism C (A only a supervisor, does not remove the ~4 s upload; B rejected).
- **affects:** modules/07-model-gateway/WARM_POOL_DESIGN.md (new + frontier fold), modules/31-frontier-bridge (courier artifact), CURRENT_STATE / HANDOFF / FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF. models.json + runtime behavior UNCHANGED (design-only).
- **revisit-if:** ITERATION 11 = build Stage-1 (the named pool manager, mechanism C) per WARM_POOL_DESIGN.md sections 6 + 9; then Stage-2 (native --models router probe + slot save/restore + a coding specialist behind its benchmark). Also outstanding: request the report audio/image/video model leads (generators #22-#25).

### D-0065 -- Fan-out iteration 12 (Verification Console verdict-persistence fix) + the iteration-13 ad-hoc follow-on (durable verdicts); Nicholas live-GUI re-confirm PASSED -> next = the EXPANDED 4-lane wave
- **date:** 2026-07-29 - **state:** locked (iter 12 fanned-out 1/1 shipped 49f7feb; iter 13 ad-hoc follow-on shipped f3c1ec7; both live-confirmed WORKING by Nicholas)

### D-0066 -- Core-docs consolidation: one live handoff, doc budgets, decision-log index, fanout-agent slots, archive/ tree
- **date:** 2026-07-29 · **state:** locked
- **decision:** (a) Exactly ONE live orchestrator handoff -- `FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF.md`, rewritten in
  place each orchestrator session (outgoing version snapshotted to `archive/handoffs/` first); the four
  overlapping handoffs (`HANDOFF.md`, `ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF_2026-07-28/-29/-29-expanded.md`) are absorbed +
  retired to `archive/handoffs/` and their Project mirrors deleted. (b) Every hot core doc gets a size
  budget and a REPLACE-don't-append rule, codified in the new `DOC_PROTOCOL.md`; the four bloated docs are
  slimmed with full pre-slim snapshots in `archive/doc-snapshots/2026-07-29/` (CURRENT_STATE 160->~27 KB,
  MODULE_ROADMAP 75->~31 KB, TOOL_MODEL_REGISTRY 92->~39 KB, REVIEW_QUEUE 29->~13 KB). (c) DECISION_LOG
  stays append-only + uncapped; a SEPARATE small `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md` (one row per decision, mirrored)
  is the routine access path — consumers read the index, then pull entries by ID. (d) Worker briefs get numbered slot docs `core-docs/fanout/FANOUT_AGENT_00N.md`
  (template + 001=GPU / 002=CPU / 003=coding), mirrored to the Project at `claude/fanout/`, so Nicholas
  dispatches a worker by pointing a fresh session at one doc; used briefs archive to `archive/fanout-agents/`.
  The retired `claude/iter11-stage1-DRAFT.md` is renumbered into `FANOUT_AGENT_001.md` (warm-pool Stage-1,
  i14). (e) `archive/` is git-tracked and indexed (`archive/ARCHIVE_INDEX.md`); nothing is deleted, only
  moved.
- **reason:** CURRENT_STATE (160 KB) and DECISION_LOG (295 KB) had grown past what any agent can ingest
  whole; `[prior]`-chain accretion duplicated the same history in three docs; four handoffs overlapped; the
  Project doc list was bloating every session's context. The most important data must forward without
  blowing up context, and nothing may be lost (Nicholas's directive).
- **alternatives:** keep accreting (unusable); move history into the Project (bloats every session; disk+git
  is canonical); a database (overkill for text docs).
- **consequences:** doc editors must follow DOC_PROTOCOL (budgets, replace-don't-append, snapshot-then-
  rewrite, index upkeep, "you bust it you slim it"); a cold-start orchestrator ingests ~60 KB of hot docs (a module session ~40 KB); doc budgets are
  actual+~10-15% and rise only via a new D-entry;
  archived content is one copy-out away; Project mirror set shrinks by 5 docs and gains DOC_PROTOCOL +
  fanout/ briefs.
- **affects:** every core doc; `core-docs/fanout/` (new); `archive/` (new); the Project mirror map
  (DOC_PROTOCOL section 8); START_HERE reading order + checklist.
- **revisit-if:** a budget proves genuinely too tight for a doc's current-truth content (raise it in
  DOC_PROTOCOL via a new D-entry, don't silently bust it); or the slot-dispatch flow doesn't match how
  Nicholas actually dispatches workers after a couple of waves.

- **date:** 2026-07-30 · **state:** locked

An off-box frontier DIRECTION review (pack `42ad8308`) was folded (`research/2026-07-30-frontier-review-self-tasking-orchestration.md`).
- **provenance / recovery:** R1a is the res.lease-primitive slice of the R1 work order from Nicholas's out-of-band provision (D-0071). The worker (`RESLEASE-R1a-split`) built the full R1a, but a **device-bridge collapse cut off its dev.ship COMMIT + `-Action report`**; the orchestrator RECOVERED — verified the uncommitted build (74/74 `-Live`), shipped via `dev.ship` (`e701328`, fail-closed sha+AST+tests), and filed the worker report on its behalf. **dev.ship GOTCHA:** it reported `committed:false` FALSELY (a post-commit git check tripped on an untracked `_to_delete/write_probe_tmp`) although `e701328` landed correctly with exactly the 6 res.lease files — logged in CURRENT_STATE Known failures.
- **frontier verdict (folded):** the trajectory review is substantially correct and **R1a-primitive-first / R1b-consumer-second is a SOUND seam** — PROVIDED R1a is treated as PROVISIONAL (protocol implemented, hardware behavior UNPROVEN), NOT as evidence the GPU hand-off is solved. **GOVERNANCE CORRECTION (applied): R1a must NOT close findings 1/13/14 — they close only after R1b.** Recommended sequence: **R1a -> R1b -> a minimal opt-in ABA baton proof -> soak -> R2 (default-ON) -> expanded #26**; strong preflight (R3) developed alongside but NOT coupled to the lease rollout; the SOAK remains the real default-ON gate. **R1b hardening the review requires:** separate THREE identities (`gpu_authority_epoch` / `resident_generation` / `exec_lease_id`), NOT one overloaded token; one scheduler-owned ATOMIC transition (no window where the old owner reacquires or the new owner gets an ordinary lease mid-eviction); explicit in-flight revocation (bounded drain -> cancel -> Job-Object tree-kill; DISCARD post-fence results); do NOT call the evictor under the state mutex; WDDM headroom = 3 stable observations ~250 ms apart after tree-exit + recheck before load, `required_vram` = measured PEAK + max(512 MiB, 10 %); only the durable supervisor starts/kills servers; the mock must be ADVERSARIAL. **R4 revised to an ABA chain** (9B plan -> generator -> 9B RESUME+verify + a mid-chain coordinator crash-recovery) to prove the orchestrator can RETURN, not just leave; a CPU-resident deterministic coordinator (not the LLM) owns the journal/lease/state; checkpoint compaction from the first proof (transcript re-ingest is ~quadratic).

### D-0073 -- Fan-out iteration 19 (single-worker core-infra wave): R1b GPU-lease-split PRIMITIVE layer shipped (res.lease 0.3.0) + a folded frontier concurrency/safety red-team -> i20 = R1b' primitive hardening
- **date:** 2026-07-30 · **state:** locked
- **decision:** i19 ran a single-worker wave (plan `fo-19-3aa34fe9`), the R1b GPU-lease-split unit (`RESLEASE-R1b-consumers`). The worker took the work order's DOCUMENTED FALLBACK: it shipped the R1b **PRIMITIVE** layer only -- `res.lease` #29 `0.2.0 -> 0.3.0` (`2d45ffe`): three-identity fencing (`gpu_authority_epoch` / `resident_generation` / `exec_lease_id` + the `authority_ok` full-tuple gate), the single scheduler-owned atomic `-Transition` (reserve -> mint epoch -> revoke pin -> drive the evictor OUTSIDE the lease mutex via a `<resource>.txn` journal -> confirm tree gone -> confirm STABLE headroom -> grant only if the reserved epoch is still current), in-flight revocation (a late/stale-epoch result DISCARDED), an adversarial MOCK evictor + crash reconcile; additive/default-off (a plain acquire/release/renew/status/list byte-identical to v0.1); **74/74** v0.1/v0.2 baseline (0 regression) + **36/36** v0.3 adversarial, green off-machine + on-box. The #7/#21 consumer wiring + the real nvidia-smi evictor + the live-GPU proof did NOT ship (model-bound, not regression-provable off-machine); a code-level adoption spec (`R1b-consumer-adoption-spec.md`) was delivered to Nicholas instead. An off-box frontier concurrency/safety **red-team** (pack `b823d9db`, GPT-5.x) was couriered + folded.
- **verification / recovery:** the orchestrator re-verified the REAL HEAD via native git at close-out -- HEAD == `2d45ffe4c8e64baa3ffe3450ea750541eec9780b` on `master`, 4 files +526/-17, trailers present; box `degraded:false`, leases dir empty. `frontier.bridge` `read-return` captured the red-team `valid` + `pack_id_match` (sha256 `f607fc88...`, 34360 bytes).
- **frontier verdict (folded):** the three identities are conceptually necessary but **NOT sufficient**, and the transition is **unsafe as ordered** -- it grants an ordinary execution lease before the new resident is loaded / healthy / published. Deepest hole: R1b fences state *transitions* under the mutex, but the dangerous *side effects* (process kill, manifest write, stop callback, server start) happen OUTSIDE it, and rejecting a stale txn at recommit does not undo a kill that already fired -> every async side effect must be fenced AT THE TARGET. **Findings 1/13/14 CANNOT close even on the planned live proof** (this VINDICATES the worker's fallback; the shipped `2d45ffe` is additive/default-off with nothing consuming `-Transition` yet, so it is safe to leave -- NO revert). Must-block changes -> the R1b' spec: (1) grant-before-ready -> start under a scheduler-only *transition capability*, publish+grant atomically only after health; (2) add `owner_incarnation_id` + `resident_instance_id` (random, never reused; target destructive ops at `resident_instance_id`, never generation/PID/port/config_key), `exec_lease_id` -> UUID -> closes the ABA hole; (3) target-fenced callbacks (CAS transition_id+epoch+instance+lease+state_version in the SAME critical section; the supervisor rejects a stop unless the immutable target matches); (4) idempotent saga journal (recommit checks transition+phase+state_version+target+epoch, not epoch alone) + distinguishable STARTING/HEALTHY_UNPUBLISHED/COMMITTED phases + commit-response idempotency; (5) Job-Object spawn-before-assignment fix (suspended-create -> assign -> resume); (6) reserve+fence = one atomic commit; + fail-closed preparation-failure semantics; + the adversarial matrix A-K. Named acceptable default-off residuals: WDDM prep-failure after sampling, empty-GPU abort, suboptimal timeouts, basic same-priority fairness, supervisor-crash service interruption, unproven long-tail -- warm-pool default-ON stays prohibited regardless. Long-run repetition / latency stay for the soak.
- **sequencing decision (Nicholas's explicit call, 2026-07-30):** i20 = **R1b'** -- a SINGLE-worker CPU (`gpu:false`) `res.lease` #29 primitive-hardening wave folding the must-block changes into `0.3.0 -> 0.4.0` + the off-machine-provable adversarial matrix (A-K mock). The #7/#21 consumer adoption (behind `-UsePoolLeaseSplit`/`-SplitLease`, default-off) + the real evictor + the live-GPU 3B<->9B swap / pin-revocation / prepared-eviction proof + the live-only tests become a LATER GPU single-worker wave. **findings 1/13/14 STAY OPEN** until that later wave; warm-pool default-ON now awaits R1b' + the consumer wave + a soak.
- **reason:** the red-team GREW the R1b remainder; primitive-first-then-consumers honors the core-infra single-worker rule (section 8) and tightens the foundation before consumers build on it. Chosen over folding the revision into the consumer wave (breaks single-worker-core-infra; higher wedge/regression risk) and over parking R1b (the primitive gate matters for warm-pool default-ON).
- **results:** R1b primitive shipped `2d45ffe` (74/74 + 36/36); red-team captured (pack `b823d9db`); i19 GPU-lane brief archived + slot 001 refilled with the R1b' brief; 0 orphans; `review_queue.jsonl` unchanged (res.lease is a non-producer). `next_iteration=20`.

### D-0074 -- Scheduled (unattended) fan-out orchestrator: evaluated, found IMPOSSIBLE on this build, DROPPED; unattended iterative fan-out deferred to the local baton-pass agent (R4/#26)
- **date:** 2026-07-30 · **state:** locked
- **decision:** evaluated a proposed scheduled (unattended) fan-out orchestrator -- one Cowork Scheduled-Task session that would run the next fan-out iteration unattended (no questions, deferring to a NEEDS-HUMAN block). RAN a live reachability gate: a throwaway one-shot scheduled task fired on-demand 2026-07-30 22:49Z. It **FAILED** -- the fresh scheduled session had **no device bridge to the local box** (no `mcp__remote-devices__*` reach to `C:\Users\just_\LifeOrchestrator-Refresh`; `device_bash`/executor/GPU unreachable; confirmed by Nicholas from the run). On this build, scheduled tasks execute in a context that cannot touch the local machine + executor + GPU the mission requires -> the scheduled orchestrator is **IMPOSSIBLE here, not merely gated**. **DROPPED** (not adopted as a core-doc); this entry is the durable note. The interactive fan-out orchestrator (`FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF.md`) stays the only way to run the loop.
- **future intent (Nicholas):** the long-term home for unattended iterative fan-out like this is the **local baton-pass agent** (R4 / `skill.orchestrator` #26) once it is strong enough -- a CPU-resident deterministic coordinator driving LOCAL models (in-bounds per D-0051), NOT a scheduled Cowork session. Revisit the unattended loop THERE, not via the scheduler.
- **boundary note:** the evaluation upheld D-0051 (the scheduled variant would not have driven other AI sessions; frontier stays human-couriered, D-0052). Two sub-questions were ratified during the eval but are moot given the drop -- recorded only in case the idea is revived on a build that supports scheduled-LOCAL execution: sub-agents-as-in-session-parallelism (in-bounds) + MODE=Auto.
- **affects:** `DECISION_LOG.md` / `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md`; `CURRENT_STATE.md` (one Unresolved-questions note). No core-doc policy file committed (the evaluated draft was delivered to Nicholas as a reference, not adopted). · **revisit-if:** Cowork gains scheduled runs that execute ON the user's computer (device bridge available to scheduled fires) AND there is reason to prefer that over the local baton-pass agent; or R4/#26 matures enough to own the unattended loop.

Shipped **res.lease 0.3.0 -> 0.4.0** (`f6df675`, 5 named module-29 files, +887/-65; ADDITIVE + DEFAULT-OFF + backward-compatible -- a plain / v0.2 / v0.3 call is byte-identical; v0.4 fields appear only on a v0.4 input or `-Action commit|fence-op`).
- **what shipped:** (1) incarnation identities close the ABA hole -- `owner_incarnation_id` (per owning-process restart) + `resident_instance_id` (per server process tree, the TARGET of every destructive op, never reused) + `exec_lease_id` as a UUID; `resident_generation` stays a human counter, never a destructive-op target; folded into `check.authority_ok`. (2) a two-phase transition = no grant-before-ready: `-Transition -TwoPhaseCommit` issues a NON-usable transition capability (the new server starts under scheduler authority, not an ordinary exec lease); `-Action commit -HealthOk` publishes + issues the first USABLE exec lease only AFTER health (STARTING -> HEALTHY_UNPUBLISHED -> COMMITTED); fail-closed prep semantics for headroom-never/fell, partial-tree, health-fail/OOM, capability-expiry, superseded. (3) target-fenced side effects: NEW `-Action fence-op` refuses a stale stop/kill/publish/release/renew/health-fail/idle-evict unless it names the exact current `resident_instance_id` (+ optional epoch / exec-lease / state-version). (4) an idempotent saga journal (`<resource>.txn`, the red-team field set) + request_id commit-response idempotency (a retry returns the SAME grant). (5) an oplock-serialized renew can NEVER resurrect a revoked lease (fixed a real resurrection race + preserved the v0.3 identity ext the prior renew dropped). (6) a durable per-resource `state_version` sequences waiters (no one-shot signal).
- **gate:** GREEN both places (cloud pwsh 7.4.6 AND on-box via the executor): v0.1/v0.2 74/74 + v0.3 36/36 (0 REGRESSION) + NEW v0.4 adversarial 45/45 (matrix A-K, mock -- ABA recovery / commit-response loss / stale side-effect matrix / superseded op / reentrant evictor / renewal-revocation race / priority-preemption liveness / partial-alloc-fail / lease-expiry-per-phase / waiter sequencing). dev.ship fail-closed all green; commit VERIFIED via native git (D-0072); 0 orphaned llama-server/python; `review_queue.jsonl` 20==20 (non-producer); leases empty; heartbeat `degraded:false`.
- **deferred (NOT faked; listed in the v0.4 suite header + README):** the LIVE-only red-team cases (real GPU OOM on a partial load, a real supervisor/descendant escape + PID reuse, real WDDM external-consumer pressure, the real 3B<->9B swap / pin-revocation / prepared-eviction proof). Job-Object contract is PRIMITIVE-side only (the mock supervisor enforces target-fenced stop; the real launcher chain = the consumer wave). The 3-obs-250ms headroom check is documented as a HEURISTIC, not a guarantee against outside allocation.
- **still open:** **findings 1/13/14 STAY OPEN** -- they close ONLY at the later **R1b CONSUMER wave** (GPU, single-worker: model.gateway #7 PoolManager + agent.local #21 governor adoption of the split behind `-UsePoolLeaseSplit`/`-SplitLease` default-off + the real nvidia-smi/eviction evictor `-EvictorMode command` + the live-GPU proof + the live-only tests), then a soak, then warm-pool default-ON. (4) #29 0.4.1 command-evictor `tree_gone` passthrough to the transition result + txn journal.
- **gate + live proof:** 0 regression off-machine (res.lease 74/74 + 36/36 + 45/45; #7 228/228 + NEW lease-split 63/63; #21 102/102 + AutoRamp 122/122 + NEW split 22/22) PLUS a FULL live-GPU proof on the 2080 Ti (evidence `modules/07-model-gateway/runtime/proof-i21/`): a 3B<->9B swap chain (no grant-before-ready, epochs monotonic, no managed-resident overlap), a second owner revoked the pin -> a PREPARED GPU (not a freed lock), revoke-during-ACTIVE-inference -> drain/cancel/tree-kill + result DISCARDED, a late stale result REFUSED by `check authority_ok:false` AND `fence-op` AND a supervisor targeted evict, fail-closed prep (`-HealthFailed` -> GPU EMPTY) = red-team case H live, real-OOM = an ENVIRONMENTAL negative (driver 591.74 spills to system RAM; measured-PEAK `required_vram` is the only real admission control), OFF==ON equality on liveA/liveB, 0 UNMANAGED orphans, queue 20==20, heartbeat `degraded:false`.
- **findings 1/13/14 = CLOSE-ELIGIBLE at the res.lease/consumer LEASE layer** (13 whole-task-lease starvation + 14 lock-order cleanly closed by the proven exec/pin split; 1 fencing closed at the res.lease layer).
- **BUT default-ON is NOT soak-only.** A PARALLEL off-box frontier SECURITY red-team of the durable supervisor + real evictor (pack `5cbe8913`, captured `.answer.md`, `read-return` valid + pack-id-match; digest `research/2026-07-31-frontier-supervisor-redteam.md`) returned **NO** with 7 structural must-fix blockers. The review predated the worker's commits, so the orchestrator VERIFIED all 7 against live HEAD `00e5912`: 6 are in code i21 never restructured (B1 spawn-before-Job-assign + nonfatal assignment; B3 one supervisor-wide Job Object + heuristic `tree_gone`; B4 pool-lock 60s steal + nonce-less release; B5 fail-open EnsureResident on the default path; B6 wedged-supervisor-still-"running" + fallback split-brain; B7 provenance-by-timestamp + weak `Test-ResidentIdentity`), 1 (B2 IPC target-fencing) is PARTIALLY addressed (the `evict` handler threads `target_resident_instance_id` + refuses a mismatch, but the target is optional + auth/replay/singleton/`shutdown` remain). Precision correction: the normal evict kills by `taskkill /T` on the exact target-fenced pid tree, NOT by closing the shared job, so B3's wrong-tree-via-job-close interleaving isn't the live kill path; the "no per-resident job / `tree_gone` heuristic" critique stands. **finding 5 (durable Job-Object custody) is RE-OPENED.**
- **consequence:** warm-pool default-ON now gates on (1) a SUPERVISOR-HARDENING wave (the 10 ranked must-fixes: per-resident suspended-create Job custody + assignment-fatal; lifetime supervisor singleton; authenticated exact-`resident_instance_id`-target IPC; no-launch-after-failed/partial-evict-or-CAS; pool-lock nonce + no stale-age steal; hard probe deadlines + `unmanaged_vram_pressure`; heartbeat-stale watchdog recovery with no live-but-unresponsive fallback; kill/reload restart reconcile; real exe/model verification), (2) an in-proc `res.lease` client (the i21 split-overhead finding: ~6-9 child-pwsh spawns/call dominate), (3) a GROWN soak (>=24h continuous supervisor, >=1000 transitions, sleep/resume, separate >=25-each fault counts, p99+max / handle-leak / lock-hold / unmanaged-pressure metrics + the 15 enumerated live tests). ## D-0077 — 2026-07-31 — Fan-out iteration 22: the Phase C two-lane CPU wave + the orchestrator cross-module reconciliation

- **decision:** Run i22 (plan `fo-22-d2c492e7`) as a TWO-LANE CPU wave on the Phase C video spine — Nicholas's
  explicit lane pick (defer the supervisor-hardening wave; skip the frontier lane; the remaining "portability"
  CPU slices are all model-bound/core-infra and cannot ride a no-GPU multi-lane wave). CPU lane
  `TRACK-STABLE-i22`: `track.objects` #33 0.1.0->0.2.0 (`b60340c`) — the frontier-reviewed STABLE-IDENTITY
  tracker as the new default `-Mode stable` (scene-boundary hard separation; elapsed-TIME aging; deterministic
  GLOBAL per-class-per-scene integer-Hungarian assignment with the lexicographic tie rule AS CONTRACT;
  two-tier IoU-then-gated-centroid association; fixed-point + canonical JSON split from diagnostics; the
  richer sample-manifest/first-class-gaps/separated-evidence schema; `score_unit millionths`; pre-first-scene
  `scene_index -1`), greedy retained byte-identical as `-Mode greedy`. Coding lane `VIDEO-TIMELINE-i22`: NEW
  `modules/34-video-timeline` 0.1.0 (`e8583d1`, arch pos 21) — the deterministic fixture-driven manifest fuse
  into ONE canonical searchable `lifeorch.video_timeline/0.1` timeline (appearance segmentation never merged
  across gaps; sampled/not-sampled/tracker-gap distinguishable; fail-closed validation; honest degradation).
  Both lanes built in DELIBERATE ISOLATION against the same design digest
  (`research/2026-07-30-track-objects-design-review.md`), ambiguities recorded in per-module SCHEMA_NOTES.md.
- **the orchestrator fold:** a cross-module smoke (REAL #33 stable canonical output fed into #34 — the check
  neither isolated worker could run) caught 2 consumer-side contract divergences: (1) #34 read
  `detection_score` as a 0..1 float and re-quantized ×1e6 — real 900000 became 900000000000 in an "ok"
  timeline (silent evidence corruption); (2) #34 refused the emitter's documented pre-first-scene
  `scene_index -1`. Fixed CONSUMER-SIDE as #34 **0.1.1** (`bad9e27`): honor the emitter's file-level
  `score_unit` declaration (verbatim integer q; float mode now FAIL-CLOSED at score > 1); accept
  `scene_index >= -1` as an opaque partition label (never an `index.by_scene` bucket). NEW recon suite 20/20
  (fixtures embed real emitter bytes) + main 138/138 (one generator-version assertion updated) + smoke rerun
  green end-to-end. The emitter's canonical bytes and shipped hashes are untouched.
- **reason:** the emitter followed the digest's fixed-point rules; the consumer followed its field-name float
  convention — both defensible readings of an under-specified digest; the consumer aligns to the emitter's
  DECLARED contract (no heuristic scale-sniffing, no emitter rework, no hash churn).
- **alternatives:** fix the emitter to emit floats (REJECTED: violates its fixed-point/no-float canonical-bytes
  determinism); heuristic value>1 scale detection without a declaration (REJECTED: ambiguous at 1, silently
  guessy); declare the wave failed and respin (REJECTED: two small additive consumer fixes, fully gated).
- **consequences:** the #33->#34 chain is PROVEN compatible on real bytes; findings/gates unchanged elsewhere;
  **standing rule adopted: parallel isolated workers building a schema PRODUCER and its CONSUMER against a
  shared design doc REQUIRE an orchestrator cross-module smoke (real producer bytes into the consumer) at fold
  before close** — green isolated gates cannot see divergent readings. New Known-failure: pwsh 7.4.6
  `[System.Array]::Sort(object[], Comparison[string])` sorts a CONVERTED COPY (silent no-op on the original);
  double-run byte-identity gates caught it. Doc debt named: CURRENT_STATE (~41 KB/34) + MODULE_ROADMAP
  (~40 KB/37) over budget -> a slim pass is a named unit for an upcoming wave.
- **affects:** `modules/33-track-objects` (0.2.0), `modules/34-video-timeline` (NEW, 0.1.1),
  `CURRENT_STATE.md`, `MODULE_ROADMAP.md`, `FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF.md` (rewritten),
  `core-docs/fanout/` (slots 002/003 used -> archived -> reset), `archive/`.
- **state:** locked. · **revisit-if:** the dense-stream decision gate lands (it may reshape #33/#34 input
  contracts); the live `#32->#16->#33->#34` composition wave starts; a #34 consumer needs `quality_score`.

## D-0083 -- 2026-08-01 -- Fan-out iteration 26 (orchestrator-only, NO worker wave): the retrieval-record/provenance CONTRACT FREEZE -- NEW core-docs/MEMORY_CONTRACT.md (record-envelope v0.1 + embedding-provider 0.2 + retriever 0.2 + catalog/eval/scale-privacy gates)

The D-0077 smoke had surfaced two concrete producer/consumer divergences (span object-vs-string; embedding skipped-input null-vs-zero). Nicholas scoped i26 to the contract freeze ONLY (no worker dispatch, no GPU).
- **decision:** freeze the shared memory/retrieval contract in a NEW versioned core-doc `core-docs/MEMORY_CONTRACT.md` -- the analog of SKILL_CONTRACT.md for the memory substrate and the ONE governing contract named for the D-0077 cross-module-smoke rule for every Wave 2+ producer/consumer split. Versions frozen: retrieval-record + provenance ENVELOPE v0.1 (the highest-leverage change -- chunks become ONE record_kind exposed via a view/adapter, so file-chunks do NOT become the universal memory abstraction); embedding-provider 0.2; retriever 0.2; plus normative catalog / evaluation / scale+privacy gates.
- **content (normative highlights):** record envelope (record_id / record_version_id / record_kind / namespace / content_hash / status / authority / sensitivity / valid_from-to / ingest-run / source_version / span+derivation / parser+chunker+extractor+schema fingerprints / token_count / embedding_space_id / parent+child edges; two-level chunk identity). Embedding 0.2: exactly one of text|texts; ordered per-input items (skipped => NO vector, never a zero vector); input/vector/failed counts; input_role; embedding_space_id (full fingerprint -- storage keys on it, not provider+dim); explicit truncation policy; float32-LE-BLOB storage; determinism as repeatability + batch-equiv + retrieval-invariance (not byte equality); out-of-place resumable re-embedding. Retriever 0.2: record+version+kind ids; span is ALWAYS an object {start,end} + derived span_label string (RESOLVES the i25 divergence); currentness+authority; per-channel lexical/vector/fused ranks+scores + fusion+index version + stable tie_break_key (retires the single opaque score). Catalog gates: schema_version+migrations; ingest-run+commit state; parser+chunker fingerprints; transactional staged current-version swap; tombstones; physical-vs-logical identity; crash/restart fault injection; exact provenance reconstruction. Staleness taxonomy (9 kinds, not one boolean). Evaluation gates: passage-level + evidence-group labels; temporal intent; negatives/no-answer; precision/nDCG/forbidden/stale/dup/diversity/provenance-VALIDITY metrics; MANDATORY lexical-vs-vector-vs-hybrid attribution. Scale+privacy gates: allowlisted roots; sensitivity_class; tested exclusions; egress + instruction-vs-evidence separation (a safety blocker for later action waves); pinned SQLite/FTS determinism; ~200 MB full-corpus CPU rehearsal.
- **adoption:** Wave 1 modules run as shipped at 0.1; each adopts 0.2 on its next NAMED revision (#35 -> embedding 0.2; #36 -> retriever 0.2 + catalog gates; #37 -> eval 0.2). Wave 2 NEW modules (repo intelligence, episode/failure schema, recorder) build to 0.2 from day one. Amending a frozen field = a new D-entry + version bump here + re-verify the affected module.
- **effect:** the "NEXT = contract freeze" flags in CURRENT_STATE + FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF are cleared; NEXT = Wave 2. NO module code changed and NO commit to any shipped 0.1 interface (adoption deferred to named revisions). Doc-debt slim pass (CURRENT_STATE / MODULE_ROADMAP / handoff / DECISION_LOG_INDEX over budget) remains the named follow-on -- i26 kept its hot-doc edits size-neutral rather than slimming.
- **affects:** NEW `core-docs/MEMORY_CONTRACT.md`; `DOC_PROTOCOL.md` (doc-set table + mirror map); `CURRENT_STATE.md`, `FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF.md` (next-action + status by replacement); `DECISION_LOG(_INDEX).md`; the memory modules #35/#36/#37 (0.2 adoption named, not yet built).

- **decision (Amendment A1, MEMORY_CONTRACT record-envelope v0.1 -> v0.1.1):** the MINIMAL, LOSSLESS amendment that touches only the non-conformant producer. (1) The envelope `status`/`currentness` is a SINGLE STRING from the s5 enum (`current` = healthy baseline) -- NOT a boolean, NOT an object; the `{state, stale_reasons, verified}` object form is RETIRED. Multiple simultaneous stale reasons -> an optional `attrs.stale_reasons`; a provenance-check failure -> the `unverified` value (no separate `verified` flag); a DOMAIN/body status (e.g. a failure's investigation state unverified|hypothesized|confirmed|resolved) stays a distinct `body.status`, never the envelope status. - **why not the alternative** (ADD `episode_stage` to the s1 enum + freeze the status OBJECT): that would widen the shared abstraction and force #35/#36/#37 changes, against the s0 amendment protocol's minimal-change intent; and the i27 fold already found the `episode_stage` records REDUNDANT (stages also live in `episode.body.stage_sequence`). The string status matches what #36 0.2 (`STATUS_ENUM`) + repo.intel already ship.

## D-0089 -- 2026-08-03 -- Fan-out iteration 31 (SELECTION-POLICY SETTLE, part 1 of 2): CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT s4 scoring PINNED to #37's canonical selpol_rrf_v1 (the #40 reference + AUTHORITY_POINTS retired)

- **context:** the i30 D-0077 fold (D-0088) shipped context_packet/0.2 and PASSED the #41->#36->#40 chain but CAUGHT a MATERIAL selpol producer/consumer divergence: #40's in-module `selpol_reference.py` and #37's canonical `lib/selpol_rrf_v1.py` select DIFFERENTLY -- relevance-primary rank-RRF vs the raw-fused-score composite; AUTHORITY_POINTS[40-320] vs AUTHORITY_RANK[1-4]; FRESHNESS_POINTS[0-200] vs a rank; excerpt-hash-only clustering vs source-MMR + display dedup; id-string vs hit-copy output. NEITHER a defect -- s4 froze the interface + stages but NOT the weights/relevance-primary/diversity/output, so P1-1's "one selection owner" was not yet realized. Nicholas approved i31 as a single #40-only CPU worker + this orchestrator s4 amendment, keeping P1-2 (pure-rank-RRF-primary) DEFERRED.
- **decision (the contract; committed NOW, before the wave, so #40 builds to it):** amend CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT s4 to PIN #37's canonical `selpol_rrf_v1`/`1.0.0` as the ONE selection policy, fixing what s4 left open:
  - **relevance-primary = the composite base** `1*raw_relevance + 3e6*authority_rank + 6e6*freshness_rank + 2e6*each descriptor match - 1e9*hard_demote - 2e7*stale_penalty`, ordered by descending `effective = base - 8e6*prior_same_source` (original retrieval rank tie-break). `raw_relevance` = the retriever's fused/lexical/score (MEMORY_CONTRACT s3), NOT rank-RRF. RRF-over-channel-ranks stays a `rrf_score` FEATURE + the fusion rule for a multi-occurrence candidate / dedup cluster ONLY. **Pure-rank-RRF as the PRIMARY sort stays the DEFERRED P1-2.**
  - **authority** = `epistemic_authority` -> AUTHORITY_RANK {authoritative/governing:4, curated:3, source_material:2, derived:1}; **freshness** = {current:3, unknown:2, stale:1, deleted/unverified:0}. #40's AUTHORITY_POINTS(40-320) + FRESHNESS_POINTS(0-200) scales RETIRED.
  - **diversity** = greedy source-MMR (`-8e6*prior_same_source`) + optional occurrence-preserving DISPLAY dedup by `chunk_content_hash` (evidence_cluster_id + occurrences[], provenance never erased); #40's excerpt-hash-only clustering (no source-MMR) RETIRED.
  - **output** = additive `select() -> { selected[]=hit COPIES, ranked[]=copies preserving channel ranks + selection_rank/selection_score(int millionths)/selection_policy_id/selected/reason_codes[]/retrieval_occurrences[]/rrf_score, features_by_candidate, omission_manifest[], stages[] }`; #40's id-string selected[] + new-row output RETIRED.
  - **ownership** = #37 owns `modules/37-retrieval-eval/lib/selpol_rrf_v1.py`; #40 IMPORTS it directly (the `selpol_reference.py` stub RETIRED), supplying `params.hard_filter` from `control_plane.permission_grants` (+ forbidden/privacy) + candidate `status` for temporal.
- **why pin #37's canonical (not #40's reference):** P1-1 names #37 the SOLE owner; the contract already DEFERS pure-rank-RRF-as-primary to P1-2; #37's canonical is byte-identical to the shipped `rerank()` baseline (its benchmark + A/B stay regression-green). Pinning it settles ownership WITHOUT changing selection results, and keeps P1-2's re-calibration + fixture regen out of this wave.
- **affects:** CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT.md (s0 pin bullet + s4 Scoring/one-owner bullets + s8 #40/#37); DECISION_LOG(_INDEX).md. context_packet stays 0.2 + selpol_rrf_v1 stays 1.0.0 (the pin ratifies existing behavior -- no version bump). ## D-0090 -- 2026-08-03 -- Adopt the full long-horizon memory architecture as the evidence-staged DESIGN TARGET (+ a seam audit + a foreign-corpus benchmark architecture)

- **context:** the DECISION_LOG_INDEX bloat slimmed today (DOC_PROTOCOL s2) was a SYMPTOM: the memory subsystem's examined-per-task cost scales with corpus size, so the assistant is robust on the first prompt but would DETERIORATE as iterative prompts + accumulating external projects pile on. Nicholas's end-state is an autonomous agent that helps manage real work for others -- it must keep accumulating projects/knowledge/decisions/procedures/failures/relationships over an indefinite operating life while ordinary working context stays BOUNDED, intelligence coming from external memory + retrieval + verified procedures rather than the 9B's parameters. A frontier memory review (RAPTOR/GraphRAG/HippoRAG/MemGPT/Soar/Generative-Agents/RETRO-grounded) proposed a full hierarchical, typed, provenance-preserving, consolidating memory. The orchestrator's initial 'build 3 layers not 10' framing was CORRECTED: for this end-state the fuller architecture is the right target, not overkill -- the discipline is DESIGN-NOW vs BUILD-NOW, not narrowing scope.
- **decision:** ADOPT the full architecture as the design target. NEW governing docs `core-docs/MEMORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` (target + Tier-0 invariants + typed memory + bounded-fanout hierarchy + multi-path query-aware retrieval + consolidation + procedural promotion + foreign-corpus intake + reconstructability + limitations + the T0-T3 evidence-staged roadmap + the 9B role) and `core-docs/MEMORY_BENCHMARK.md` (validation: real self-verifying OSS corpora, an INDEPENDENT deterministic mutation/withholding harness, executable + hidden ground truth, the 8-stage lifecycle measures, functional-equivalence + correct-abstention scoring, NO synthetic project replica, the 9B is not the oracle). Gap analysis: `research/2026-08-03-memory-architecture-seam-audit.md`.
  - **the load-bearing principle:** a DETERMINISTIC skeleton (ids/versions/edges/nodes/splits/namespaces/routing/budgets/staleness) + PROVISIONAL model content (synopses/claims/reflections/procedures) that always reconstructs to a LOSSLESS substrate and is VALIDATED before trusted. A bad model pass can only emit a view that fails validation; it cannot corrupt evidence. This reconciles intelligent + deterministic + always-provisional + cheap-to-re-run.
  - **evidence-staged, not speculative:** DESIGN the whole thing now; BUILD the anti-deterioration foundation before substantial autonomous external work; ACTIVATE scale mechanisms on measured need. Tier 0 (design-now invariants + seam repairs) / Tier 1 (anti-deterioration foundation) / Tier 2 (operational memory formation) / Tier 3 (advanced scale). Full bitemporal claims + global map-reduce are Tier 3 (deferred), but present contracts must preserve clean SEAMS for them.
- **urgent Tier-0 corrections (lock-in risk if deferred; seam audit s3):** (1) namespace as a HARD retrieval/partition boundary (today a soft boost -> cross-project bleed bakes into every derived layer); (2) protect the HIERARCHY seam (#36 schema + retriever channel model + selpol/compiler must admit a node layer + shortlist-and-descend without a rewrite; stop hardening flat-top-k); (3) a per-`task_id` WORKING-MEMORY store seam (today foreclosed -> the mechanical cause of iterative-prompt deterioration); (4) current-over-stale as a real retrieval MODE + supersession-aware ranking + a `contradicts` edge; (5) keep the retriever channel model OPEN + reserve a query-classification stage. Items 1-2 are true lock-in; 3-5 are foreseeable foundation.
- **strengths to build ON (seam audit s1, [CLEAN]):** lossless substrate + retained originals; stable ids/versions; A2 provenance modes + derivation edges + reconstructability; rebuildable derived views; schema evolution; the bounded context packet + expansion seam. The target EXTENDS these, it does not replace them.
- **affects:** NEW `MEMORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` + `MEMORY_BENCHMARK.md` (+ DOC_PROTOCOL doc-set table [30 KB / 14 KB] + mirror map); NEW seam-audit digest; CURRENT_STATE (Phase); PROJECT_DIRECTION / ARCHITECTURE_MAP / MODULE_ROADMAP (pointers); the DECISION_LOG_INDEX budget rises 12 -> 20 KB (append-only, ~90 decisions + growing; per-row discipline stays the guard). `MEMORY_CONTRACT.md` + `CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT.md` stay the FIELD-LEVEL authorities, amended (not replaced) to satisfy the Tier-0 seams. No module code this decision. The i31 SELECTION-POLICY SETTLE wave (fo-31-eca37c08) is in flight; its fold is now D-0091 (this entry took the D-0090 that D-0089 forward-referenced for the wave).
- **effect:** the memory subsystem now has a north-star + invariants + evidence gates. Memory waves draw from `MEMORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` s10; Tier 0 (the seam repairs) is the immediate memory-side work once i31 lands.
- **state:** locked. * **revisit-if:** the benchmark shows a Tier-0 invariant is unachievable on this hardware; a seam classified additive proves foreclosed at build time (amend + re-audit); scale evidence pulls a Tier-3 item earlier.

## D-0092 -- 2026-08-04 -- Fan-out iteration 32 (Tier-0 MEMORY-ARCHITECTURE seam repairs, part 1 of 2): MEMORY_CONTRACT A4 + CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT i32 amendment enforce/reserve the Tier-0 seams; 3-lane CPU conformance wave + a frontier red-team dispatched

Nicholas chose Option A (combined: author the amendments AND dispatch the conformance wave this iteration) + the optional frontier red-team in parallel.
- **decision (part 1 -- the contracts, THIS commit):** amend the two field-level authorities, ADDITIVE + backward-compatible. `MEMORY_CONTRACT.md` **Amendment A4** (record/retriever half): (U1) `namespace` is an ENFORCED hard retrieval filter with an all-hits-match assertion (was a soft rank boost); (U4) `current_only` is a real retrieval MODE (hard-exclude non-`current`), supersession is rank-affecting, NEW `contradicts` edge; (U2) reserved-additive `node` record_kind + `member_of_node`/`child_of_node` edges (the flat catalog admits a node layer via a `schema_version` bump, NO rewrite); (U3) reserved-additive `working` record_kind (per-`task_id`, isolated, non-evidence, non-control); (U5) the retriever channel set is FROZEN OPEN (channel is data). `CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT.md` **i32 amendment** (packet/selection half): namespace hard-filter in selpol (`hard_filter_namespace`; the soft project-match bonus retired) + the compiler passes namespace to selpol + retriever; `current_only` hard stale filter + `superseded_demote`; the FOURTH `working_memory` packet region (task-authoritative for STATE, not execution authority, not evidence; render control_plane->task_input->working_memory->evidence); a deterministic query-classification stage stamping `query_class` that drives selpol's temporal mode. `non_execution:true` UNCHANGED. Doc budgets raised (MEMORY_CONTRACT 24->28 KB, CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT 22->25 KB; DOC_PROTOCOL s2 table) to hold the new normative content (a contract-prose slim pass is named doc-debt).
- **wave (part 2, DISPATCHED -- 3-lane CPU, GPU lane skipped [no model until the fold], plan `fo-32-<id>`; docs:[]; MaxParallel 3):** #36 artifact.search 0.2.0->0.3.0 (retriever namespace hard-filter + all-hits-match; `current_only` mode; reserve node/member_of_node/child_of_node/contradicts/working via a `schema_version` 2->3 additive migration; owns gate tests 1 namespace-isolation + 2 schema-evolution); #37 retrieval.eval + `selpol_rrf_v1` 1.0.0->1.1.0 / eval 0.3->0.4 (selpol `hard_filter_namespace` + `current_only` hard filter + `superseded_demote` + `query_class` temporal mode; channel model kept open); #40 context.compiler 0.3.0->0.4.0 (pass namespace hard filter; query-classification stage; reserve the `working_memory` region; import selpol_rrf_v1 1.1.0; re-assert provenance-expansion gate test 3). ## D-0093 -- 2026-08-04 -- Establish PROCESS_BACKLOG (cross-cutting process/doc-hygiene router) + a capture rule; the mechanical commit gate, not the prose checklist, is the forcing function

- **context:** i32 surfaced that deferred CROSS-CUTTING process/tooling/doc-hygiene fixes (a doc-budget/index-brevity commit gate; a subagent delegation seam; the long-drifting hot-doc slim) had NO durable home: module follow-ons live per-module in MODULE_ROADMAP, runtime review items in REVIEW_QUEUE, but process debt only ever appeared as handoff "named residuals" (rewritten every session -> drift) or scattered "doc debt" mentions (deferred i25->i32). The trigger was the D-0090/91/92 DECISION_LOG_INDEX re-bloat (verbose entry-summaries pasted as routing rows, one session after the index was slimmed) plus three hot docs over budget (handoff 51 KB/24, CURRENT_STATE 53/34, MODULE_ROADMAP 44/37). Root cause: brevity/size discipline is prose-only and erodes under load -- DOC_PROTOCOL s9 step 5 ALREADY mandates a size check and it was skipped this session.
- **decision:** create `core-docs/PROCESS_BACKLOG.md` -- a terse ROUTER (one row per open item: id, one line, a deterministic Trigger, a D-ref; detail lives in the D-entry, same discipline as DECISION_LOG_INDEX) with an explicit capture rule (anyone deferring a cross-cutting process fix adds a row + a D-entry, not a handoff residual). Wire it into DOC_PROTOCOL s2 (budget 8 KB) + s8 mirror map (-> claude/). Capture three items: **PB-1** the doc-budget/index-brevity commit GATE (the mechanical pawl -- fail a core-docs commit that busts a budget or a >160-char index cell); **PB-2** the RESERVED subagent delegation seam (DELEGATION_PROTOCOL + bounded index + hygiene-brief templates; reserve now, build when subagents exist AND >=3 recurring judgment-hygiene tasks accrue -- Tier-1-style, do NOT build a router to a missing capability); **PB-3** slim the over-budget hot docs.
- **the honest forcing function:** NOT a new prose checklist step (s9 step 5 proves that fails). The durable pawl is PB-1, the mechanical gate, which forces the matching item at commit time; the backlog is the memory, the gate is the alarm. PB-1 is therefore the highest-priority process item.
- **alternatives:** stuff the items into MODULE_ROADMAP (rejected -- it is 5.8 KB over budget; growing it to hold "fix doc bloat" is the anti-pattern); a D-entry alone (rejected -- not discoverable as a consolidated list, exactly the "swept under the rug" failure Nicholas named).
- **affects:** NEW core-docs/PROCESS_BACKLOG.md; DOC_PROTOCOL.md (s2 table + its own budget 10->11 KB + s8 mirror map); the doc-commit gate (PB-1, when built); FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF + CURRENT_STATE + MODULE_ROADMAP (PB-3 slim targets, deliberately NOT touched here to model the discipline).
- **state:** provisional. * **revisit-if:** PB-1 lands (flip the forcing-function reliance to the gate); subagents deploy (PB-2 activates); the hot-doc slim happens (PB-3 -> DONE).

## D-0094 -- 2026-08-04 -- Adopt a SUNSETTING process-hygiene mandate + a blind sealed-check; correct the budget model to proportional/density + re-layer (not static caps)

- **context:** i32's process-design thread (D-0093 + the discussion) converged on three things. (1) Attention/discipline is unreliable at EVERY model tier (demonstrated this session at the frontier tier), so prevention must be MECHANIZED, not left to a "self-healing" agent -- cure needs the same unreliable attention it is meant to fix. (2) The builder must obey the same deterministic-skeleton contract as the memory it builds; the docs are the FIRST corpus the memory system should own, and process-hygiene + memory MERGE at scale. (3) STATIC doc budgets set at the starting size are the "ceiling at the start" anti-pattern -- they fire on legitimate growth (100 -> 1000 decisions), train dismissal, become theater (why DOC_PROTOCOL s9 step 5 was skipped + three hot docs sit over budget). Nicholas asked for a way to keep improvements from being permanently forgotten WITHOUT front-loading everything.
- **decision:** (a) NEW `core-docs/PROCESS_MANDATE.md` -- a SMALL, time-boxed, SELF-DELETING mandate (mandate 01: opened i32, sunset i40) addressed each session by a cheap status pass + an expiry check (the DEADLINE, not per-iteration progress, is the forcing function), producing a MANDATORY metastability REPORT at sunset (per-item ship/defer/drop; the four control dimensions stale-prevention/recovery/memory/local-controls; a metastability YES/NO; NO -> propose mandate 02; YES -> start the sealed check). (b) A BLIND SEALED-CHECK -- at a YES verdict, seal machine-checkable health PREDICATES not opened for `sealed_check_offset` (7) iterations, then a CHECK session evaluates them deterministically; the seal resists teach-to-the-test. (c) CORRECT the budget model (folds into PB-1): DENSITY (bytes/state) is the bloat metric; budget = density_cap x state_count x headroom (proportional, recomputed); a RE-LAYER trigger at the bounded-read threshold (max a hot doc ingested whole, pegged to the 9B) -> shard + index-the-index + retrieval (#36) -- NOT static caps, NOT endless slimming.
- **the honest refinement (vs the raw discussion):** the mandate + sealed check must carry DETERMINISTICALLY-CHECKABLE predicates or they become theater like every prose rule before them. The mandate is a deterministic trigger (expiry = a machine-checkable condition); the sealed check is a regression test (measurable predicates, evaluated blind). Judgment stays ONLY where a gate can detect-but-not-fix, and there the gate FORCES the fix.
- **knobs (defaults, ratifiable):** sunset_iteration 40; sealed_check_offset 7; bounded-read threshold ~40 KB/hot-doc (peg to the 9B's real usable context -- the one number most worth setting); index density cap ~160 chars/cell.
- **affects:** NEW PROCESS_MANDATE.md; PROCESS_BACKLOG PB-1 (corrected to density/proportional/re-layer); DOC_PROTOCOL (table + mirror map -- NOTE its STATIC budget table is now a STOPGAP that PB-1 replaces with proportional budgets); FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF (hybrid: the i32 wave-complete review + the mandate); the i32 fold/close (PB-3 slim); DECISION_LOG_INDEX (this row).
- **state:** provisional. * **revisit-if:** the i40 sunset report (metastability YES/NO); PB-1 lands (flip budgets to proportional + retire the static table); the sealed check runs.

## D-0095 -- 2026-08-04 -- Fold the frontier Tier-0 amendment red-team (pack 159e9cb5): NO-GO to close i32 as-written -> i33 NAMESPACE-CLOSURE + SUPERSESSION-HARDENING

- **context:** the i32 Tier-0 amendments (A4/i32, D-0092) shipped their conformance wave (envelope-level namespace/current_only/seams; #36/#37/#40 DONE, HEADs ef667f7/26224d7/31ebdf7). The parallel frontier red-team (pack 159e9cb5) reviewed the AMENDMENT TEXT vs MEMORY_ARCHITECTURE + the seam audit. Digest: research/2026-08-04-tier0-amendment-redteam.md.
- **verdict (FOLDED): RE-AMEND, THEN SHIP.** NO-GO to fold/close i32 as-written; CONDITIONAL GO after a NARROW re-amendment. The architecture + prioritization are substantially correct; the fixes are contract clarifications, NOT new capabilities. Two defects force a re-amendment BEFORE close: (1) SAFETY-CRITICAL -- U1 namespace is an ENVELOPE filter, not an end-to-end information-flow boundary (derived-record namespace laundering; diagnostic/omission metadata leak across ranked[]/features/stages/occurrences/omission/expansion-hints/eval-hooks; per-hop + traversal gaps; working-state reopening by task_id alone; task_input.namespace wrongly called authoritative vs P0-1); (2) correctness -- U4 supersession is candidate-set-dependent (a predecessor stays `current` when its successor is absent from the pool; the s5 enum has no literal `superseded`).
- **decision:** open **i33 = NAMESPACE-CLOSURE + SUPERSESSION-HARDENING** -- an ADDITIVE re-amendment of MEMORY_CONTRACT + CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT (the committed A4/i32 are INCOMPLETE, not wrong): (1) namespace CLOSURE = effective_allowed = intersection(request, grant); all-packet-visible-fields scope-check; namespace-homogeneous derivations across transitive provenance; sanitized rejection (violation_count + failed_closed, details to a local security log; abort-on-violation); conjunctive task_id+namespace working-state access; ONE canonical predicate + error policy across retriever/selpol/compiler. (2) candidate-INDEPENDENT effective_current from the catalog/graph + a literal `superseded` state + chain invariants (acyclic, no cross-ns supersession, branching->conflicted). (3) conditional-provenance retriever hit shape (by provenance_mode) + candidate_role(navigation|evidence) + a multi-stage-retrieval seam. (4) the working-state STORE contract (state_version/CAS/lifecycle/budget/promotion; "continuity-authoritative"; search() rejects record_kind=working). (5) query_class-vs-temporal_intent split + classifier policy versioning + composite/unclassified. Then re-conform #36/#37/#40 + run the D-0077 mixed-namespace fold smoke on the HARDENED contracts (now testing the leakage paths). An as-written D-0077 baseline MAY run first; the CLOSE is gated on i33.
- **affects:** MEMORY_CONTRACT + CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT (i33 re-amendment); modules #36/#37/#40 (i33 re-conformance); the D-0077 fold; FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF (NEXT = i33); NEW research/2026-08-04-tier0-amendment-redteam.md. The i32 envelope-level wave STANDS as the foundation.
- **state:** provisional. * **revisit-if:** i33 lands the re-amendment + the hardened fold smoke passes -> i32/i33 close; any later change reopens the namespace-closure model.
