The load-bearing defect (P0): the draft claimed shortlist-and-descend PRESERVED recall while letting a bounded beam PRUNE branches on LOSSY structural synopses (a bounded entity_union/lexical_descriptor/centroid can omit the feature that makes a required leaf relevant; deterministic construction makes the miss reproducible, NOT impossible). Same shape as D-0095 (an envelope-level design hiding a defect).
- **decision (part 1 -- the contracts, THIS commit):** ADDITIVE + backward-compatible; schema_version 4->5 (an un-built tree = zero nodes = today's exact flat behavior). MEMORY_CONTRACT **Amendment A6** (record/retriever/catalog half): the load-bearing SAFE-PRUNING invariant (a navigation value may POSITIVELY prioritize but NEVER NEGATIVELY exclude a branch without a deterministic channel-specific NO-FALSE-NEGATIVE proof at the pinned snapshot; a STALE synopsis never prunes; else expand/switch-channel/flat-fallback/needs_expansion/abstain); H1 the `node` BUILD fields (structural synopsis + a SUFFICIENT-STATISTICS vector aggregate, NOT a mean-of-normalized-child-centroids; immediate-input provenance); H2 three SEPARATED state axes (evidence status / topology state / navigation-synopsis freshness) with MONOTONIC generations + CAS regen (kills the stale-clear ABA/lost-update race); H3 hierarchy IDENTITY (hierarchy_id/kind/builder_policy/tree_version/root) + ATOMIC tree-version publication (avoids the "one tree per namespace" foreclosure); H4 the MINIMAL balanced deterministic BULK-BUILD + rebuild/flat-fallback triggers (live incremental split -> i35/Tier-2); H5 write-time + transitive namespace HOMOGENEITY over every aggregate + nav-visible object (SAFETY-CRITICAL); H6 AUTHORIZATION-BOUND frontier ops shortlist/descend (effective_allowed_namespaces + hierarchy_version + corpus_snapshot -- no confused-deputy descend, SAFETY-CRITICAL). CONTEXT_PACKET_CONTRACT **i34 amendment** (packet/selection half): V1 shortlist-and-descend as a multi-stage retrieval PLAN (nodes route via candidate_role=navigation, NEVER evidence); V2 SAFE-PRUNING enforcement; V3 RETRIEVAL-COMPLETENESS fields (a hierarchy miss != proved absence; DISTINCT from evidence coverage); V4 hierarchy in packet identity + one pinned tree_version/compile; V5 navigation-vs-evidence closure. Doc budgets raised (MEMORY_CONTRACT 38->48, CONTEXT_PACKET 29->37 KB; DOC_PROTOCOL s2) for the A6/i34 normative content (the contract-prose slim stays named doc-debt, PB-3).
- **wave (part 2, TO SCOPE + DISPATCH -- 3-lane CPU, GPU skipped; docs:[]; MaxParallel 3):** #36 artifact.search 0.4->0.5 (the node layer + balanced bulk-builder + generations/CAS staleness + write-time+transitive homogeneity + authorization-bound frontier ops + atomic tree-version publication + the safe-pruning channel predicates + schema 4->5 migration + the hierarchy gate tests); #37 retrieval.eval (navigation-cost + hierarchy-PATH-recall + end-to-end PACKET-EVIDENCE-recall + adversarial scale fixtures + the real ~200MB rehearsal); #40 context.compiler (the shortlist-and-descend PLAN + safe-pruning enforcement + retrieval_completeness + navigation_refs; import #37 READ-ONLY). The orchestrator runs the D-0077 hierarchy fold smoke at close.

## D-0109 -- i39 frontier as-built RE-REVIEW: p0_1_gate_status walked back to incomplete (over-claimed)

- **Date:** 2026-08-06
- **Decision:** The couriered frontier AS-BUILT RE-REVIEW of the i39-completed P0-1 gate (pack `b2b1e5fb`, read-return captured/valid; `research/2026-08-06-i39-p01gate-recheck-redteam.md`) returned **FAIL -- `p0_1_gate_status=pass` is OVER-CLAIMED**. Walk `p0_1_gate_status` back to **`incomplete`** (the D-0107 pattern, one wave later). `build_status=build_complete` + `activation_status=prohibited` UNCHANGED; deny-by-default HOLDS (the orchestrator D-0077 fold-i39 18/18 + the independent #43 run_suite 308/308 re-run stand; A06 denies every authentic packet; `non_execution:true` holds; nothing action-capable). Contract s7 amended (D-0109 fold-block + footer).
- **The 7 findings (all SUITE-BUILD; none reopens a frozen field) -> i40 unit-0:** (1) completion is not immutably bound at issue time (optional cc.packet_id / issue-time-binding checks; a permit issued with no contract records None, a contract can be inserted later) -> require cc.packet_id present+matching + issue-time binding id/version/digest match + a NO_COMPLETION_CONTRACT sentinel + late-insertion vectors; (2) Boundary-D injects drift PRE-claim not POST-claim (the oracle mutates before execute(); the epoch recheck is skipped when issue_snapshot is absent; effects copy authorized_effect_set instead of applying through the captured handle) -> a post-claim hook + mandatory snapshot + handle consumed by the effect ledger + a fault per epoch; (3) the role matrix OMITS the frozen `manifest` + `working_memory` sinks (26/26 is against a narrowed 13-sink list, not the 15 frozen sinks); (4) GrantView declares a `limits` array + intersection rule but the matcher reads only `max_quantity` and A23 uses only the manifest ceiling -> implement+pin the min-intersection algebra (or amend `limits` away) + golden vectors; (5) the 0.9.0 chain is authentic at A06 but SYNTHETIC beyond (corpus_version=None bypasses A07; the grant snapshot is swapped; the trace/wm are reduced to two booleans) -> a suite-owned EXACT context_packet/0.2 adapter preserving all carriers/identity + a test-only overlay changing only non_execution; (6) the 141-row matrix is broader but not independently decisive -- no_path is source-pattern matching (not an ast call-graph over all modules), A36 checks only absence-of-leak (not mandatory audit emission), Boundary-B rows are presence assertions -> a real call-graph + mandatory-audit assertion + real-render Boundary-B; (7) the 21-file review pack is not independently runnable (omits action_authz/__init__.py, tests/{harness,fixtures_suite,properties,fuzzer,report}.py, the packet fixtures + report bundle -> run_suite ImportError) -> ship a complete runnable tree.
- **Reason:** the audit loop working as designed -- the worker's self-reported `pass` is not the gate; the independent frontier as-built review is. The P0-1 gate has now been over-claimed TWICE (i38->D-0107, i39->D-0109). PROCESS lesson for i40: build EXACTLY to the frontier's exact-closure list BEFORE claiming pass, and re-run the frontier as-built review BEFORE the orchestrator ratifies s7.
- **Consequences:** the P0-1 gate stays a DESIGN build (activation prohibited; non_execution holds; nothing action-capable). i40 = SUNSET (the mandatory PROCESS_MANDATE-01 metastability report is due first) + unit-0 the 7 findings + PB-5 (#37) + PB-3 (hot-doc slim). Bundle unit-0 with PB-5 into one #43+#37 wave if lanes allow.
- **Affects:** #43 (i40 unit-0), ACTION_AUTHORIZATION_CONTRACT s7, CURRENT_STATE, FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF, DECISION_LOG_INDEX (D-0108 pass claim marked superseded).
- **State:** locked.

Contract s7 amended (the D-0113 fold-block + an updated freeze-state footer).
- **Accepted as closed by the reviewer:** F3 (the all-15-sink role matrix, 30/30) + F6 (a real stdlib-ast call-graph no_path; A36 exactly-one-bounded-event + deletion/corruption faults; Boundary-B explicitly defense-in-depth) + the quantitative limit-intersection portion of F4 + the post-claim/terminal portion of F2. Reviewer-observed on the transported tree: behavioral 334/334, mutations 67/67, fuzzer 400/0, oracle 149 rows / 0 not_run, role 30/30, completion 17/17, views 48/48 -- the build substance is accepted; the holes below block ratification.
- **The 5 remaining findings (all SUITE-BUILD; none reopens a frozen field) -> the i41 #43 unit:** (1) PermitStore completion binding must be WRITE-ONCE (`record_completion_binding` is an unrestricted overwrite; the getter returns the live mutable dict -- the NO_COMPLETION_CONTRACT sentinel / original binding can be replaced after issuance; require write-once per permit_id + reject any second recording + immutable/defensive-copy representation + sentinel-overwrite / binding-overwrite / getter-mutation / duplicate-recording vectors); (2) the captured target must be a trusted **TargetHandle CAPABILITY the effect applicator CONSUMES** (the executor copies `authorized_effect_set` and tags entries with the `resolution_proof_digest` STRING; require a distinct handle object, an applicator API that requires it, the ledger generated from the handle-bound applicator result, one-shot/observably-consumed handles, and a KILLED blind-copy+tag mutant); (3) a **LOSSLESS 0.9.0 adapter** (`adapt_packet_view` drops identity fields -- compiler_version, selection_policy, query class, temporal intent, classifier policy, ns-closure data, working-state version, retrieval-plan digest, consumer profile, selected rvids, budget, omission digest -- and carriers -- retrieval provenance, selection structures, evidence structures, control plane, transport accounting; require complete-packet or canonical-bytes preservation + per-identity-field mutation properties [any change alters the preserved identity or fails closed] + round-trip equivalence; overlay flips ONLY non_execution); (4) **OPERATIONAL top-level GrantView closed-set enforcement** (an unknown top-level grant field is currently ACCEPTED by the matcher; validate the exact field set/types before matching, reject unknown/missing/mistyped, vectors, pin the operational validator); (5) **COMPLETE pack transport** -- the i40 pack omitted `WORK_ORDER.md` (digest 36e713da..; an ORCHESTRATOR pack-authoring omission -- the file exists on-box; the reviewer's empty-dir run exited 1 at finding_7 and selfverify FileNotFoundError'd): future packs are GENERATED from the suite's own required-file manifest and extracted+run from an EMPTY DIR before couriering.

M2-D removed the over-claim failure mode; what remains is the ordinary build->review->build loop.

## D-0114 -- i41: per-lane worker model tiering (Nicholas directive); fanout retained over serial consolidation

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** Adopt PER-LANE WORKER MODEL TIERING (handoff s12 = the operating rule): the DEFAULT worker lane runs **Sonnet 5 High** when the unit is exact-spec'd, suite-gated fail-closed, and not ratification-critical; a lane ELEVATES to **Opus 4.8 Extra** on any trigger (the module previously failed a frontier review / was walked back; frozen-contract or core-infra semantics; design-vs-exact-closure work; two in-lane gate failures) and de-elevates after two consecutive first-pass ships; **Fable 5 Max** is reserved for the orchestrator seat (while its allocation lasts) and for orchestrator-INLINE execution of premium-demand SMALL-DIFF units (a fresh premium worker would re-pay full orientation as novel input at the top rate); the orchestrator states a RecommendedModel per lane in every check-in + slot brief; roster/prices are refreshed each session. The FANOUT ARCHITECTURE IS RETAINED -- work is NOT serialized into the orchestrator: fresh narrow worker contexts + the orchestrator-as-independent-grader boundary (M2-D's enforcement point) stay; only micro-units (doc touch-ups, scope notes) run orchestrator-inline.
- **Reason:** the weekly usage cap is the binding constraint (~1 lost work-day/week). Verified API prices 2026-08-07 ($/MTok in/out; cache-read 0.1x input): Sonnet 5 = 2/10 (3/15 from Sept 1); Opus 4.8 = 5/25 (Opus 5 lists at the SAME price); Fable 5 = 10/50. The lane verification structure (fail-closed suites, double-run byte-identity, orchestrator independent re-runs, frontier ratification) is model-independent, so execution-capability downgrades on routine lanes are low-risk; project history (#43 x3 review rounds; the i38/i39 over-claims happened on STRONG models and were caught by process, not model strength) shows REWORK, not per-token price, dominates token cost -- so the ratification-critical lane is never downgraded.
- **Alternatives:** all-Opus everywhere (rejected: pays 1.7-2.5x on lanes whose failure modes the structure already catches); serialize all work into the orchestrator (rejected: destroys the independent-grader boundary that caught D-0107/D-0109, floods the orchestrator context with implementation churn, and re-pays that ever-growing context every turn at the premium rate).
- **Consequences:** i41 Lane A (#43 0.5.0) ELEVATED to Opus 4.8 Extra (fourth ratification round); the i42 M2-A build lane recommended Sonnet 5 High with elevate-on-first-failure; handoff s12 records the rule + roster.
- **Affects:** FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF (s12), fanout slot briefs (Recommended-model header line), check-ins, PROCESS_MANDATE M2-E context (orchestrator-inline is the ORCHESTRATOR itself acting, not a spawned subagent -- outside the open M2-E question).
- **State:** provisional (revisit-if: a Sonnet lane's first-pass failure rate makes re-runs eat the savings; the Fable allocation ends; prices/roster change; the M2-E ruling reshapes delegation).

This is the FIRST ratified pass of the P0-1 gate; M2-D is satisfied (this session names the pack id).
- **The review (independent):** the reviewer reconstructed all 47 courier-pack files into a fresh dir and re-ran `run_suite.py` (exit 0, 364/364; mutations 69/69; oracle 152 not_run=0; role 30/30; completion 21/21; views 64/64; bundle `3b5d62f426143b29`) + `selfverify.py` (exit 0, VERIFIED:True). ## D-0125 -- i45 acceptance CORRECTED (Nicholas): LRAP v1 is NOT a phenomenological pass even on its built scope -- action+flag visibility without agent-role possession; the D-0124 "scored PASS" is walked back

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision (correction, Nicholas -- the D-0050/D-0064 audit authority):** D-0124 recorded the five-fixture walk as a "SCORED HUMAN PASS." Nicholas CORRECTS this. He CAN see the machine's inconsistency FLAGS and infer their meaning (he reads "a pack was dropped at step 4" and that it is flagged inconsistent) -- but that is NOT a PHENOMENOLOGICAL pass. To audit phenomenologically he must be able to ADOPT THE AGENT'S ROLE: independently judge what SHOULD happen (e.g. WHICH pack to drop), do it himself, and COMPARE to what the agent did to see what went wrong. He CANNOT, because: (1) he has NO initial prompt / raw input to judge against (the D-0123 raw-prompt front-step gap); (2) the RATIONALE / LOGIC / underlying MECHANISM the agent used is not surfaced -- RECONCILE shows a machine consistency VERDICT (F1: verdict-only, no judgment), not the agent's reasoning; (3) there may be agent-viewable CONTEXT made OPAQUE to him -- so he is NOT seeing "exactly what the agent sees" (the AUDIT_PIPELINE possession property). In his words: LRAP v1 is "I can see the actions taken without seeing the rationale, logic, or the underlying mechanism, and there may be agent-viewable context made opaque to me, incapacitating me from adopting the role of the agent to make a comparative determination."
- **Correction of D-0124:** "five-fixture human SCORED PASS" OVER-STATED it -- Nicholas can READ the machine-flagged inconsistencies, not phenomenologically CATCH them by adopting the agent's role. D-0124 is WALKED BACK on that characterization. The machine result (87/0/0, five-fixture 0 FP/FN, D-0122) and the RENDERING correctness are UNCHANGED and stand; the over-claim was Claude's, from a chip label -- the D-0107/D-0109 pattern recurring, this time at the HUMAN gate (recorded so the discipline is visible).
- **The finding (a P9 / possession gap, not a rendering defect):** LRAP v1 is action+flag-legible but NOT phenomenological even on the BUILT (assembly-side) cases -- the SAME class as the original D-0120 finding (expert-forensic vs phenomenological), at finer grain: a verdict-backed FLAG is not the human RECONSTRUCTING intent and comparing. There is a DESIGN-LEVEL tension: F1 (RECONCILE = machine verdict only, deliberately anti-over-claim) is exactly what STARVES the rationale/agent-view surfacing that P9's adopt-the-agent's-role needs. The next increment must resolve it -- surface EXACTLY what the agent saw (possession; incl. any currently-opaque context) + the per-step RATIONALE + the initial prompt, and let NICHOLAS make the comparative judgment, WITHOUT the widget substituting its own semantic judgment.
- **Consequences:** i45's BUILD stands (widgets/08 SHIPPED + machine-verified; a real legibility step). Its ACCEPTANCE is corrected to: technical PASS + Nicholas can SEE/infer the flagged actions, but the PHENOMENOLOGICAL gate (P9 / D-0064-in-full) is NOT met even on the built scope -> it stays the audit program's open target. The next increment now = adopt-the-agent's-role POSSESSION (exactly-what-the-agent-saw + rationale/logic + initial prompt) + the raw-prompt FRONT step + the LIVE ride-along + the output-side reconciliation, re-scoped so the human makes the COMPARATIVE determination himself. AUDIT_PIPELINE next_increment/current_tier + PB-4 + CURRENT_STATE + handoff corrected. No new commit to widgets/08; mandate-02 countdown unchanged (45/2).
- **Affects:** AUDIT_PIPELINE (current_tier + next_increment), CURRENT_STATE (audit-surface bullet + widgets/08 note + Last updated), FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF (acceptance clause), PROCESS_BACKLOG (PB-4), DECISION_LOG_INDEX (row + D-0124 corrected-annotation). CORRECTS D-0124.
- **State:** locked.

## D-0126 -- interpretability POSER pinned as the ungated, read-only, non-blocking next audit increment (Nicholas): per-element '?' -> pop-up chat, local-9B explanation + follow-ups; information-only invariant; NO design/red-team/legibility gate

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision (Nicholas, the D-0050/D-0064 audit authority):** the next audit increment is an interpretability POSER embedded in the LRAP surface -- a `?` beside any element opens a POP-UP CHAT that starts with a local-9B (strong tier) plain-language EXPLANATION of that element and lets Nicholas pose FOLLOW-UP questions / seek clarification live. It ships UNGATED: no design-first review, no red-team, no blocking legibility self-test, no acceptance gate. Rationale (Nicholas): it is PURE INFORMATION -> Nicholas IS the red team (adequate technical knowledge to catch incongruent info); it can WRITE NOTHING, so bad output is a non-issue -- he just informs the frontier agent for revision himself; it must not impede functionality -> FAIL-SILENT (failure shows nothing); and shipping SOONER is preferable because the poser is itself a tool for generating refined feedback on the whole audit SURFACE, so gating/delaying it costs that feedback loop.
- **The information-only INVARIANT (what makes it ungate-able -- recorded so it stays true):** the poser hooks in in NO way other than (1) READ the element's context (element schema + what the agent saw + the run data) and (2) EXPLAIN it + take FOLLOW-UPS. It writes NO state (no memory, no pipeline, no surface state, no files), has NO control surface, and its output is ADVISORY (Nicholas's to judge; drives no automated decision). Any future change that breaches this bundle (e.g. letting the poser tag / verify / mutate anything) RE-OPENS the gating question and must not be made silently.
- **The one mechanical constraint (NOT a gate -- it is what makes `fail-silent` TRUE):** the 9B call routes through the EXISTING gateway/executor lease path (never a second, direct model-invocation path), so a hung/OOM/contended query REAPS and the poser shows nothing, instead of holding the resident-model GPU and wedging the box (D-0055/56). `Fail silently` only holds if the failure cannot take the box down. Prompt-injection via audited run data collapses to the same already-accepted `info might be bad` case (bounded, human-caught, changes no state).
- **On the legibility proxy (corrects my prior over-mechanization):** the 9B's per-element comprehension stays a SIGNAL NICHOLAS MAY USE (an element the model can't explain from its tailored feed is a candidate illegibility he can choose to flag), explicitly NOT an automated ship-gate. Making it a blocking gate would substitute an automated proxy for HIS legibility judgment -- the same `don't let the machine judge for you` principle as F1/P9. Non-blocking keeps him the judge.
- **Relation to D-0125:** the poser DELIVERS the possession/rationale gap from the ergonomic end (per-element, on-demand context via the pop-up), and building it FORCES the per-element `sufficient context bundle` that possession needs. It is now the pinned FIRST next increment; the raw-prompt FRONT step + LIVE ride-along + OUTPUT-side reconciliation follow.
- **Consequences:** AUDIT_PIPELINE next_increment re-led by the poser (ungated; information-only invariant; gateway plumbing; non-blocking legibility-signal); PB-4 + CURRENT_STATE next-pointer + handoff next clause updated. No gate ceremony -> implementation may proceed directly. Fully IN-BOUNDARY (local 9B, in-session; no frontier). mandate-02 countdown unchanged (45/2).
- **Affects:** AUDIT_PIPELINE (next_increment), PROCESS_BACKLOG (PB-4), CURRENT_STATE (audit next-pointer + Last updated), FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF (next clause), DECISION_LOG_INDEX (row). Builds on D-0125.
- **State:** locked.

## D-0127 -- LRAP interpretability POSER SHIPPED (D-0126 built): widgets/08 `9f99495`, ungated + read-only + fail-silent; cloud 104/0/3 + Windows -Live 119/0/0

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision (ship):** the D-0126 poser is BUILT + committed to `widgets/08-live-run-audit-pathway/` (`9f99495`) -- a per-element `?` ("Ask (local 9B)") that opens a modeless chat seeded with the element's CONTEXT BUNDLE (authored INTENT + ACTUAL input/output + RECONCILE verdict/identities, a PURE projection of `Get-LrapModel`) and takes follow-ups; the 9B EXPLAINS the instrument + recorded facts and is FORBIDDEN to judge whether the run is correct (the F1/P9 line; a green verdict never means correct; advisory). NEW: `LrapPoser.psm1` (pure core: elements -> bundle -> prompt -> runtime-guarded request/answer protocol), `Invoke-LrapPoserQuery.ps1` (the out-of-band worker), `tests/mock-poser-gateway.ps1` (the no-GPU stand-in). CHANGED: `Show-LiveRunAuditPathway.ps1` (+ the `?` pop-up + `SELFTEST_POSER_OK`), the harness (+25 poser assertions).
- **The information-only INVARIANT HELD (the ungate-ability condition, D-0126):** the widget only READS the model + writes request/answer files under `runtime\poser\` (Assert-UnderRuntime-guarded) and SPAWNS the worker DETACHED -> the widget process makes NO model call and holds NO lease (`SELFTEST_READONLY_OK` still green). The ONE model call is out-of-band over `model.gateway` #7 / `res.lease` #29 on `llm.strong.qwen3p5-9b`. FAIL-SILENT on error / empty output / timeout (both fail branches tested).
- **Verified:** cloud gate 104/0/3 (Linux, run in-session) + `-Live` 119/0/0 (Windows WinForms self-test via the executor, incl. `SELFTEST_POSER_OK` + the read-only + fail-silent guarantees). The REAL 9B generation is exercised live by clicking Ask (mocked in tests; inherently GPU/live). AST-parse + ASCII-guard passed on all 5 shipped scripts.
- **Consequences:** the poser is DONE -> the audit `next_increment` advances to the remaining D-0125 set: (1) the raw-prompt FRONT step (step-1 INPUT P2 -> upstream emission); (2) the LIVE ride-along (audit-tag launch + per-step pause/unpause; A2.2); (3) the OUTPUT-side instruction<->output reconciliation. Each is design-first -> red-team-gated (the poser was the ungated exception, D-0126). AUDIT_PIPELINE next_increment + current_tier + PB-4 + CURRENT_STATE + handoff advanced. mandate-02 countdown unchanged (45/2; a widget ship, not a core-doc mandate action). Fully IN-BOUNDARY (local 9B, in-session).
- **Affects:** AUDIT_PIPELINE (next_increment + current_tier), CURRENT_STATE, FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF, PROCESS_BACKLOG (PB-4), DECISION_LOG_INDEX. Builds on D-0126.

## D-0128 -- LRAP poser LIVE-CLICK FIX (D-0127 follow-up): the mock-green ship had two real-integration defects the first real click surfaced; fixed + self-test hardened. widgets/08 `cc3dd81`

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision (correction + fix):** D-0127 shipped the poser mock-green (cloud 104/0/3 + -Live 119/0/0) and claimed the real 9B is "exercised live by clicking Ask". Nicholas's FIRST real click surfaced two defects the MOCK could not: (1) the DETACHED spawn -- `Start-Process -WindowStyle Hidden` from the STA WinForms event thread SILENTLY did not launch the worker (the pop-up hung on "asking..."; no request reached the gateway; no -gw dir, no answer); (2) EMPTY 9B output -- `qwen3.5-9b` reasons in a `<think>` block and does NOT honor `/no_think` on this build, so at 1280 tokens it truncated mid-thought (finish_reason=length, `content` empty).
- **Fixes (widgets/08 `cc3dd81`):** (1) native `System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo` launch (UseShellExecute=false, CreateNoWindow), fail-silent on a launch throw, AND the self-test EXTENDED to drive the REAL detached path (SELFTEST_POSER_OK now spawns the worker detached with the mock gateway + polls) -- closing the coverage gap that let a SYNC-only self-test pass a broken live path; (2) the worker token floor raised to 4096 so the 9B finishes reasoning AND emits the answer; pop-up deadline 300s, status "~1-3 min". Verified LIVE on the box: a real element now returns a grounded plain-language explanation (ok=true) -- e.g. Step 1 -> "turn a raw request into a normalized task, query class, and temporal intent...".
- **Lesson (recorded):** a MOCK-backed gate proves the GLUE, not the INTEGRATION; the D-0127 "exercised live by clicking" line was premature (mock-green != live-green). The self-test now exercises the real detached spawn; the live 9B ANSWER QUALITY is still the human's to judge. (Same honesty discipline as D-0124 -> D-0125.)
- **Consequences:** the poser now works end-to-end live. next_increment UNCHANGED (raw-prompt FRONT step + LIVE ride-along + OUTPUT-side reconciliation); no current_tier change (CURRENT_STATE/handoff already read "poser SHIPPED"). mandate-02 countdown unchanged (45/2).
- **Affects:** DECISION_LOG_INDEX (row). Corrects the D-0127 "exercised live" claim.
- **State:** locked.

## D-0129 -- LRAP poser LIVE NO-OP: ROOT CAUSE found + fixed (the dotnet-tool apphost pwsh trap); D-0128's "spawn fixed" was itself premature. widgets/08 `da8a05e`

- **Date:** 2026-08-09
- **Decision (root cause + fix):** every real click hung on "asking..." because the query WORKER never ran. Root cause (found via added `_launch`/`_worker` breadcrumbs, NOT guessed): `launch.bat` runs pwsh via the dotnet-tool APPHOST shim (`%USERPROFILE%\.dotnet\tools\pwsh.exe`), so the widget process's host image is `dotnet.exe`; the widget resolved its spawn pwsh from `(Get-Process -Id $PID).Path` = `dotnet.exe` and spawned `dotnet.exe -File Invoke-LrapPoserQuery.ps1` -- a NO-OP (dotnet.exe is not pwsh): no worker, no -gw dir, no answer. The mock self-test passed only because it ran under the real `.store` pwsh.exe; D-0128's ProcessStartInfo change did NOT fix this (the pwsh PATH, not the spawn API, was wrong).
- **Fix (`da8a05e`):** `Resolve-LrapPwsh` now searches for a REAL `pwsh.exe` (`Get-Command pwsh -All` + Program Files / LocalAppData), prefers a self-contained `.store` / Program Files exe, and NEVER uses the `dotnet.exe` apphost host or a WindowsApps alias; the real pwsh is threaded to the worker via `-PwshPath` (both paths); the worker guards its OWN gateway spawn (rejects a non-pwsh host). Best-effort breadcrumbs + a Start-Process fallback added.
- **Correction of D-0128:** D-0128 said the SPAWN defect was fixed (ProcessStartInfo) -- it was NOT; only the 4096-token CONTENT fix from D-0128 stands. The spawn is fixed HERE.
- **Verified (headless, on the box):** `Resolve-LrapPwsh` returns the real `.store` pwsh.exe; a worker spawned through it calls the gateway with real pwsh and returns ok=true (mock); the real 9B returns a grounded answer at 4096 (D-0128); `-Live` 119/0/0 unchanged. **LIVE-CLICK confirmation** (Nicholas re-launches + clicks Ask -> a real 9B answer in the pop-up) is PENDING.
- **Lesson:** three "fixed/verified" claims (D-0127, D-0128, here) ran ahead of the real environment. A mock proves the GLUE; the box proves the INTEGRATION; only the human's click proves the WHOLE path. next_increment UNCHANGED (front step + ride-along + output side).
- **Affects:** DECISION_LOG_INDEX (row). Corrects D-0128 (spawn).
- **State:** locked.

## D-0139 -- i49 closing patch (Nicholas): DECISION_LOG_INDEX growth-policy correction -- the routing catalog may grow losslessly under per-entry density; stop degrading rows to hold a static byte ceiling; scalable selective decision retrieval durably deferred

- **Date:** 2026-08-12
- **Decision:** Correct a narrow process defect surfaced at the i49 close (rows shortened SOLELY to hold the 20 KB whole-file index ceiling). PRINCIPLE: preserve the information now, optimize which part is loaded later -- the complete decision routing catalog MAY grow, the orchestrator working context must NOT, and those are separate constraints no longer solved by degrading the former to protect the latter. CHANGES: (1) `ops/audit/doc-commit-gate.py` -- `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md` is now an explicit **CATALOG** kind (growth-exempt): NO total-byte budget reject; the 40 KB D-0094 re-layer trigger becomes a WARN (`relayer_catalog_40kb`), not a reject, for it; per-row density (~160-char cell / <=200-char row, WARN) + the `[prior]`/stacked-last-updated accretion tripwires + EVERY OTHER doc budget reject remain fail-closed. (2) `DOC_PROTOCOL.md` s2 -- the index row re-labelled growth-exempt routing catalog (no KB cap; re-layer-pending ~40 KB). (3) `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md` header rule 6 rewritten: do NOT shorten/delete a valid row to hold a whole-file byte limit; per-row discipline is the only size guard; at ~40 KB re-layer (PB-6), not slim. (4) the FIVE rows compressed at the i49 close solely for the ceiling (D-0130/0131/0133/0134/0135) restored to their pre-i49 wording, each within the ~160-char cell rule (D-0133 keeps `mechanism-not-exercised` but drops the non-routing `floors clear, swap-stable` parenthetical to stay <=200). `DECISION_LOG.md` remains the authoritative, complete, append-only ledger, unchanged in role. The index now sits ~20.5 KB (past the former ceiling) with no failure.
- **Deferred (PB-6, NOT built in i49):** a Decision Memory Re-layer / selective decision routing capability. Target -- separate (a) the canonical lossless ledger (`DECISION_LOG.md`); (b) the complete routing catalog (compact-but-meaningful per decision; may grow indefinitely; need not fit context); (c) a SELECTIVE retrieval / index-of-index that returns only decisions plausibly relevant to the current task -- candidate signals: affected modules/components, architectural plane/domain, current-vs-superseded/folded status, decision type, authority/status, lexical relevance, vector/semantic similarity, graph relationships, temporal relevance, dependencies, known failures, prior retrieval usefulness, explicit decision-to-decision supersession/fold edges (MULTIPLE compensating channels, NOT embeddings alone); (d) hierarchical scaling (bounded fanout / index-of-indexes) so the hot routing surface grows in DEPTH not linearly -- the ordinary orchestrator receives a COMPILED relevant decision set, not the whole catalog. Superseded/folded decisions stay retrievable (reconstruction, regressions, architectural oscillation, causal diagnosis, precedent, prior-alternatives comparison) but normally COLD. Preserve a future path to a provenance-linked recurring-pattern / reflective-failure memory (A solves X causes Y -> B solves Y causes Z -> return to A) that summarizes the recurring tradeoff while linking the underlying decisions. This MUST reuse and specialize the already-governing memory doctrine (`MEMORY_ARCHITECTURE.md`: bounded hot sets + bounded-fanout hierarchy + query-aware retrieval + fast/slow paths + supersession-aware records + lossless canonical substrate + rebuildable derived views + selective context compilation; M2-C / docs-into-memory re-layer), NOT a parallel architecture; PB-6 records the unresolved integration/build work, it does not duplicate the design.
- **Verification:** (1) a syntactically valid new index row pushes `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md` past the former 20 KB ceiling WITHOUT a total-byte failure (the D-0138 + D-0139 rows do; index ~20.5 KB); (2) per-row density + accretion tripwires remain active; (3) `DECISION_LOG.md` authoritative + unchanged in its append-only role; (4) no historical routing rows auto-deleted/compressed for aggregate size; (5) restored rows accurate + within density; (6) the gate STILL rejects unrelated over-budget core-docs (gate unit tests green); (7) PB-6 durably records the re-layer with an explicit trigger; (8) repo clean + normal close/mirror completed.
- **Non-goals (this patch):** no decision-hierarchy build, no DB migration, no `artifact.search`/`context.compiler` redesign, no PCB A/B reopen, no N4 re-freeze, no #40 beam-width, no AUDIT increment, no general documentation re-layering, no i50 delay.
- **i50 handoff:** the decision-index re-layer is NOT the i50 frontier unless Nicholas redirects; i50 returns to the orchestrator bootstrap / handoff-consolidation + memory-comprehension thread. Note carried: "static-cap defect corrected; routing catalog now grows under per-entry density; scalable selective/hierarchical decision retrieval durably deferred (PB-6), to ride the existing memory/re-layer architecture when scheduled."
- **Affects:** `ops/audit/doc-commit-gate.py` (+ its tests), `DOC_PROTOCOL.md` s2, `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md` (header rule 6 + 5 restored rows + D-0139 row), `PROCESS_BACKLOG.md` (PB-6), `FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF.md` (i50 note).
- **State:** locked.

## D-0140 -- i50: the N4 migration-gate BAR RE-FREEZE ratified (Nicholas) -- the frozen s7 efficiency rules are replaced; a fresh legacy-vs-PCB gate is now stageable; legacy stays default until it runs

- **Date:** 2026-08-12
- **Decision:** Nicholas RATIFIED the N4 bar re-freeze as recommended (digest `core-docs/research/2026-08-12-i50-n4-bar-refreeze-proposal.md`; record `modules/44-project-map/eval/results/N4_BAR_REFREEZE.md`). The frozen `I47_EVAL_PACKET` s7 **efficiency** rules -- dead post-CD-1 (D-0136 F1/F2/F3/F4) -- are REPLACED for the next gate run (the s1-s6 mechanics and the s7 **quality** floors (a)-(d) are UNCHANGED; the packet bytes are untouched, the supersession recorded not edited in): (1) **BOOT (absolute):** `BOOT_PACKET renders <= 20,000 B` (the module HARD guard) AND carries the CD-1 OPERATIONS canon, pointer-backed, degrade-LAST; boot-total (packet + dispatch + step-0) is REPORTED, not a pass/fail gate. Retires the arithmetically-unreachable `0.5 x A-narrow` ratio (F2: CD-1 grew the packet to carry the canon the i47 run was faulted for lacking, so the pre-canon ~13 KB bar could never be met by a complete packet). (2) **TOTAL (with the query surface):** `B total <= 0.7 x A total`, where B total counts the **query OUTPUT bytes** for content served by a #44 query op and whole-file bytes only for a genuine raw open; A counts whole-file (unchanged -- legacy has no query surface). Restores the 0.7x intent under accounting that credits the N1 L2 narrative queries (F1: the whole-file proxy counted B's 651,374 B raw-store fallback -- harvest 478,784 + tool source 90,116 + arch-positions 82,474 -- now bounded queries at ~5.7 KB / ~7.7 KB each). (3) **VOID (PCB-insufficiency):** efficiency VOID iff B opens a legacy/handoff doc to obtain an answer NOT already PCB-cited earlier in the same ledger; a corroboration open does NOT void but its bytes still count toward total. Replaces the binary RT2-F9 (F3: it nuked a thorough agent's cross-check even when the PCB answered first). The separate `B total > 0.9 x A` void-(b) is RETIRED as redundant with the 0.7x total bar.
- **Reason:** the i47 bars measured a pre-N1 world; N1/N2/N3 (i49, D-0138) made the deep narrative, frontier detail, and verb table bounded queries, so a coherent gate must credit query output and stop hanging a migration decision on an unreachable boot ratio. Re-freezing BEFORE the next run (not softening after a fail) keeps the M2-D / D-0107/D-0109 anti-over-claim discipline: the new bars can still FAIL, and the projected ~0.35 x A total (i48 T3 re-accounted) is the PCB winning the metric, not the metric loosened onto a loss.
- **Alternatives:** total bar at parity `B <= A` (rejected as the ratified bar -- too weak vs the ~0.35x projection; kept as the conservative fallback) or `0.5 x A` (rejected -- thin headroom for a heavier fresh task); keeping binary RT2-F9 (rejected -- re-imports F3); dropping the void (rejected -- loses fail-closed on a real PCB gap); an absolute boot-TOTAL hard gate (rejected for the packet-only HARD bound + report). All three s7 calls set to the recommended option.
- **Consequence:** a fresh legacy-vs-PCB migration gate is now STAGEABLE against coherent, pre-frozen bars -- but was NOT run in i50 (a separate, later iteration stages + runs it). Until it runs and passes, the migration stays CONDITIONAL and the **LEGACY handoff remains the default orchestrator bootstrap**.
- **Affects:** DECISION_LOG_INDEX (row), `modules/44-project-map/eval/results/N4_BAR_REFREEZE.md` (new -- the re-freeze record beside the I47/I48 results), `core-docs/research/2026-08-12-i50-n4-bar-refreeze-proposal.md` (new -- the ratified digest), FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF s0/s3/s4/s11 (rewritten; outgoing snapshot archived), CURRENT_STATE (migration-gate line + next-action + last-updated).
- **State:** locked.

## D-0141 -- Cap exhaustion on a CUMULATIVE surface triggers RE-LAYERING, not indefinite compression (Nicholas directive); the D-0139 correction generalized project-wide into one consolidated re-layer program

- **Date:** 2026-08-12
- **Decision:** Generalize the D-0139 DECISION_LOG_INDEX growth-exempt correction into a GOVERNING RULE for every project knowledge surface. **Invariant:** when legitimate information growth collides with a hot-context cap, PRESERVE the information, INDEX and re-layer the representation, and BOUND what is activated -- never make permanent semantic degradation the scaling mechanism; a static cap is early-warning + enforcement that should increasingly act as a RE-LAYER TRIGGER, not a permanent demand that ever-growing information occupy the same bytes forever. (1) **No global budget removal** -- most hot docs stay bounded (budgets catch accidental bloat); the exception is an inherently cumulative / demonstrably-growing class (decision routing/history, iteration ledgers, module/capability + tool/model catalogs, contracts/architectural inventories, research catalogs, failure histories, procedure/skill catalogs, provenance/history indexes, other accumulating registries). (2) **Cap pressure = a re-layer signal** (repeated slimming to admit valid info; compressing old-but-useful routing already within density; chronic near-cap operation; growing opaque shorthand; dropping useful distinctions for aggregate size; archiving without a retrieval path; a growing NUMBER of bounded docs read together; a router that scales with the corpus; bootstrap cost rising as doc objects are added). (3) **Two axes** -- VERTICAL (one doc grows -> shard/index) + HORIZONTAL (the number of docs grows -> index/route across the collection); the asymptotic requirement is that NEITHER bytes-per-doc NOR docs-examined-per-ordinary-task scales linearly with total knowledge. (4) **Target per class:** lossless canonical backing + a typed indexed representation (IN the memory hierarchy, not another mandatory Markdown router) + a bounded-hot current/router view + selective typed retrieval + a preserved expansion path to authority; superseded/folded material goes COLD (retrievable via explicit/supersession/diagnosis/regression/precedent queries), not deleted; preserve a path to promote recurring cross-history patterns (A->B->A oscillation, recurring failures, reopened settled choices, recurring cost/reliability tradeoffs, procedural regressions) into provenance-linked failure/reflective/procedural memories. (5) **ONE consolidated program reusing the two engines already being built** -- the PCB (#44) for the HORIZONTAL bootstrap-doc axis + the memory subsystem (#35-43) + M2-C + MEMORY_ARCHITECTURE for the VERTICAL cumulative registries -- NOT a parallel architecture, preferring the shared generic catalog over per-class bespoke routers. Register + target arch: `core-docs/research/2026-08-12-knowledge-surface-relayer-program.md`; router PROCESS_BACKLOG PB-7 (SUBSUMES PB-6 + absorbs PB-3's >40 KB clause).
- **Reason:** i50 is a live instance -- the handoff (an iteration ledger) sat at 99% and admitting the D-0140 lines forced collapsing the i38/i39 ledger rows ("repeated slimming to admit valid info"); the doc-health monitor shows systemic pressure (PROJECT_DIRECTION 118%, ARCHITECTURE_MAP 109%, MEMORY_ARCHITECTURE + CURRENT_STATE ~100%, handoff 99%, seven more contracts/registries in the 90s; 37 research docs, 22 core-docs, 120 archived files, a 615 KB decision log). Indefinite compression trades evidentiary fidelity + routability for bytes -- the wrong scaling mechanism.
- **Consequence:** a re-layer is "done" only when complete info stays recoverable, routine tasks read a bounded relevant subset, adding irrelevant history / many new doc objects does NOT materially raise routine context/orientation cost, cold info stays discoverable, provenance permits expansion, and no progressively harsher compression is required as knowledge grows. NON-DISPLACING -- records deferred work + stops destructive over-compression; does not derail the active PCB/bootstrap/memory sequence. First increment = PB-6 (decisions); the PCB gate (D-0140) is the horizontal migration already in flight.
- **Affects:** DECISION_LOG_INDEX (row), `core-docs/research/2026-08-12-knowledge-surface-relayer-program.md` (new), PROCESS_BACKLOG (PB-7 added; PB-6 subsumed; PB-3 clause absorbed), DOC_PROTOCOL s2 (cap-as-re-layer-trigger rule), FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF s4 (deferred-menu (c) re-labelled).
- **State:** locked.

## D-0142 -- i51: the fresh legacy-vs-PCB migration gate RAN under the D-0140/N4 bars -- VERDICT NO-GO (quality floor (b) breached + total-ingest bar missed); legacy stays the default bootstrap; i52 = the fix wave (Nicholas directive)

- **Date:** 2026-08-14
- **Decision:** the i51 orchestrator staged + ran the full A/B/C1/C2 gate (Nicholas: EVAL_SHA = HEAD `ef70a57`; task T1; full rigor; coin-flip SWAP -> Candidate-1 = B) and applied the frozen I47 s7 quality rules + the D-0140/N4 efficiency rules verbatim. **Quality: floor (b) BREACHED** -- B under A by exactly 1.0 (C1/C2 mean, both adjudicators independently 3-vs-4) on constraint adherence (cited deductions: the D-0064 live-GUI-confirm rule softened to "ship not blocked on it"; the mandated audit red-team gate marked OPTIONAL; the w08 rider absent from the very w08 lane it routes to; K5 budgets/commit-gate absent) -- and floor (a) tripped under the stricter of the two adjudicator readings of adapted-K6 (mandate-sunset state never stated; NOT contradicted). **Efficiency: BOOT PASS** (packet 16,943 B <= 20,000 + CD-1 canon), **TOTAL MISS under both accountings** (primary literal N4: B 547,901 B = 2.74 x A 200,205 B; charitable sensitivity: B 220,602 = 1.12 x A 197,065; bar 0.7x), **VOID not tripped** (B's legacy-surface opens were PCB-cited-first corroboration or boot_read/stale-routed). **VERDICT: NO-GO -- the LEGACY handoff remains the default orchestrator bootstrap.** Record: `modules/44-project-map/eval/results/I51_RESULTS.md` (+ packs/verdicts/dispatches/materials byte-verbatim, raw sha256s quoted there; MANIFEST-i51.sha256 alongside).
- **Reason:** the run was decisive on mechanism, not noise. F1 task-class dependence: T1's answer lives in prose governing docs the PCB does not serve query-granular (N1 covered module purposes/SCHEMA_NOTES only), so B paid the boot layer ON TOP of A's prose corpus -- the PCB wins map-native tasks (i48 T3 re-projection ~0.35xA) and loses prose-governing tasks. F2 currency lag: 5 doc-only commits after the i49 fold left the shipped map stale; its own stale flags + boot_read routed B to re-open CURRENT_STATE + handoff whole (~58 KB). F3 canon coverage: every scored B constraint deduction is an item the BOOT_PACKET does not carry (D-0064 rule, K5 gate/budgets, mandate-sunset, poser/rider state, red-team-gate strength). ## D-0144 -- 2026-08-14 -- N8 re-run protocol RATIFIED AS AMENDED (Nicholas): C pin = Opus 4.8 Extra; dual-counter accounting; two-class scope guard

- **Decision:** ratify N8 (the migration-gate re-run protocol re-freeze) as follows. Calls 1-4 AS PROPOSED: two-class task pool (T-map + T-prose, both arms run both, GO requires every bar met on BOTH); listings charged at emitted bytes + the identical dispatch line; staged-not-read transfers charged in full + disclosure; bounded/sliced reads charged at RETURNED bytes for both arms. **Call 5 REPLACED:** C1+C2 pin = **Opus 4.8 Extra** (the project's default frontier tier); a runtime model mismatch is RECORDED and **STOPs adjudication before the C round begins** -- no silent substitution, no mid-run adaptation; escalation to **Fable 5** permitted ONLY if explicitly justified AND frozen at staging BEFORE either arm runs; **Opus 5 is NOT the default fallback/escalation tier** (marginal gain over 4.8 Extra at ~2x token price -- effectively never used here); A/B pin stays `claude-opus-4-8`, miss = record + STOP pre-dispatch. **AMENDMENT 1 -- dual-counter accounting:** every arm ledger records TWO counters, never collapsed: retrieval/materialization bytes (what the system caused to be transferred/staged/listed/retrieved/materialized) and model-visible bytes (what actually entered the agent's readable context); the N4 0.7xA pass/fail bar continues on charged retrieval bytes (pre-ratified metric unchanged); both counters reported side-by-side. **AMENDMENT 2 -- scope guard:** T-map/T-prose are MINIMUM LOAD-BEARING classes for THIS migration decision -- not a claim that orchestrator work has exactly two classes, not a canonical taxonomy. Frozen rules: `modules/44-project-map/eval/results/N8_RERUN_REFREEZE.md`.
- **Reason:** (Nicholas) C-tier price/perf: Opus 4.8 Extra sufficient -> use it; Sonnet 5 where recommended for ordinary work; Fable 5 only for genuinely difficult judgment; Opus 5 ~2x tokens for marginal gain. Dual counters: caches, prefetch, local indexes, background decomposition, embeddings, materialized records mean a cheap deterministic layer may soon retrieve ~500 KB internally to hand the frontier model ~8 KB -- excellent architecture a single undifferentiated byte count would permanently punish; do not confuse how much the computer touched with how much expensive intelligence had to consume (the F4 partial-read distortion writ large). Scope guard: temporary test constructs in this project have a habit of becoming architectural terminology.
- **Alternatives:** pin C = opus-5 (rejected: cost/benefit); keep single-counter ingest (rejected: punishes the deterministic retrieval infrastructure the project exists to build); >2-task pool now (rejected: two classes suffice for THIS decision; more is future work, explicitly not implied).
- **Consequences:** the fresh two-class gate is STAGEABLE once N5-N7 are verified landed; from the next run on, every eval ledger separates retrieval cost from model-visible cost.
- **Affects:** `modules/44-project-map/eval/results/N8_RERUN_REFREEZE.md` (NEW, the frozen rules); `research/2026-08-14-i52-n8-rerun-refreeze-proposal.md` (ratification header); the next gate staging + dispatch texts; worker-model tiering guidance (FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF s12).
- **State:** locked.
- **Revisit-if:** the model roster changes materially before the next staging (re-pin AT staging, recorded); the dual counters prove ambiguous on a real ledger (tighten definitions at the next staging, BEFORE dispatch, never mid-run).

## D-0148 (2026-08-14) -- i54 follow-on: close-refold reaffirm clobber fixed (one-process restamp) + i55 PB-7 dispatch-ready

**close-refold reaffirm clobber (found while validating the D-0147 `-Harvest` fix).** The `-Harvest` fix was necessary but not sufficient. `ops/close-refold.ps1` reaffirmed one entity per SUBPROCESS, each doing load-map -> mutate one entity -> write the WHOLE map file; run as rapid sequential subprocesses via the executor, a later reaffirm reloaded a stale `meta.json` (missing the prior write) and CLOBBERED earlier restamps -- so most restamps silently did not persist (the i54 close needed a manual one-process workaround; only load-bearing stragglers surfaced via STALE_LOAD_BEARING at validate). **Fix:** `modules/44-project-map/reaffirm_batch.py` applies the whole orchestrator-reviewed spec in ONE process (synchronous in-process load/write accumulates correctly), reusing `project_map.op_reaffirm` UNCHANGED -- no edit to the tested map functions, no #44 version bump/digest re-pin. `close-refold.ps1` now calls it once (resolving python as `Invoke-ProjectMap.ps1` does) instead of the per-entity subprocess loop. Proven: a 6-entity stale-injection differential persisted every restamp; the live i54-follow-on re-fold reaffirmed the staled docs with NO manual workaround -> 0 stale at HEAD.

**i55 PB-7 dispatch-ready.** `FANOUT_AGENT_001` filled with the PB-7 knowledge-surface re-layer DESIGN brief (design-first -> red-team, Opus 4.8 Extra; mirrored to `claude/fanout/`). PB-7 inputs verified present: the PROCESS_BACKLOG PB-7 + PB-6 rows, `research/2026-08-12-knowledge-surface-relayer-program.md`, D-0141. The retrieval-byte monitor stays the i55 slot-002 lane (Sonnet). The frontier/overlay/handoff already point at PB-7 as i55 lane 1 (D-0147). SP3/M-03 remains OPEN (Nicholas-deferred); P0-1 activation FROZEN; SEALED_CHECK_47 retained.

## D-0152 (2026-08-15) -- i58 SET to the universal derived-front-door wave: START_HERE's cap event FIRES PB-7; raw docs stay lossless backing but stop being the ordinary read path

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Decision:** At the i57 close, `START_HERE.md` crossed its 6 KB budget (5992/6000 after the D3 doctrine edit) and was held under only by abbreviation. Nicholas ruled that abbreviation is the ANTI-PATTERN, not the fix: START_HERE crossing its cap -- a cumulative hot surface's repeated slim-to-admit -- SATISFIES PB-7's activation trigger (D-0141). The PCB renderer's slim-ladder (96->72->56-char description truncation, entry->count collapse, authority-list trim to 40, section compression at the 20 KB packet ceiling) is COMPRESSION mechanized inside the generator -- it violates PB-7 G5 "spill, never compress" and is NOT index-of-indexes scaling. Therefore i58 is set to the **universal derived-front-door wave**: **i58** freezes + red-teams the universal derived-front-door contract (raw documents remain the lossless canonical backing but STOP being the ordinary consumption path; every hot surface becomes a bounded GENERATED front door that spills to cold, never compresses -- generalizing the PCB BOOT_PACKET pattern to all cumulative surfaces). **i59** = the root migration: START_HERE reduced to a stable ~0.5-1 KB bootstrap KERNEL (how to verify/rebuild the PCB + open the generated packet; NO iteration, active-work, module-roster, or changing project state -- the one exception to "everything generated," so a broken generator still leaves a trustworthy recovery entrypoint) + the PCB packet. **i60+** migrate CURRENT_STATE, handoff/frontier, module/tool catalogs, backlogs, gotchas, tests, and research incrementally. i57's shipped unit is NOT broadened (`d16cdc0` stands); this close only removes duplicated dynamic material from START_HERE (the active-work counter + the live-status pointer), records the trigger, and redirects i58.
- **Reason:** the boot surface is the load-bearing recovery path; solving its cap pressure by abbreviation degrades the one artifact a fresh or recovering session must trust. A generated front door that SPILLS (bounded hot + count-asserted + lossless-backed + selectively retrieved) is the PB-7 answer already proven on the decision surface (PB-6, D-0151); the cap event is the signal to generalize it. The AUDIT review_due i58 (D-0147) still gets its required cadence check but is explicitly NON-DISPLACING -- this bootstrap-surface failure outranks the audit increment.
- **Alternatives:** keep abbreviating START_HERE per close (REJECTED -- the anti-pattern; degrades the recovery entrypoint and re-trips every close); do the full START_HERE kernel reduction now in i57 (REJECTED -- broadens the shipped unit; the careful kernel reduction is i59, after the contract is frozen + red-teamed); treat the cap as ordinary doc-slim under PB-3 (REJECTED -- PB-3's >40 KB clause is absorbed by PB-7/D-0141; this is a bootstrap re-layer, not a slim).
- **Consequence:** i58 = freeze + red-team the universal derived-front-door contract (design-first -> red-team, per PB-7); i59 = root migration (START_HERE kernel + PCB packet); i60+ = class-by-class migration. PB-7's trigger is RECORDED as FIRED (`PROCESS_BACKLOG` PB-7). AUDIT review_due i58 NON-DISPLACING. This close: START_HERE deduped (not abbreviated); CURRENT_STATE "Next expected action" + FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF s4 redirected to i58; no change to the i57 shipped unit. SP3/M-03 OPEN; P0-1 activation FROZEN.
- **Affects:** `core-docs/START_HERE.md` (dedup: active-work counter + live-status pointer removed), `core-docs/CURRENT_STATE.md` (Next expected action + Last updated), `core-docs/FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF.md` (s4 frontier + header), `core-docs/PROCESS_BACKLOG.md` (PB-7 trigger FIRED; PB-6 DONE), `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md` (row); the PCB overlay frontier + BOOT_PACKET (N7 close-refold). The renderer slim-ladder is flagged as the anti-pattern to REMOVE during the i58/i59 migration (not this close -- the i57 unit is unbroadened).
- **State:** locked.

## D-0153 (2026-08-15) -- i58 CLOSED: the universal derived-front-door contract FROZEN + 3-adversary red-teamed (7 breaks -> hardened s8 authoritative); i59 amended (kernel + cold-boot card)

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Decision:** i58 executed the PB-7 universal derived-front-door wave (D-0152, trigger fired i57): FROZE the universal derived-front-door contract and RED-TEAMED the generalization (orchestrator-inline, D-0119). Frozen set (3 research digests, each <=10 KB): `research/2026-08-15-i58-universal-front-door-contract.md` (the law -- raw docs = lossless canonical backing but STOP being the ordinary read path; every cumulative hot surface -> a bounded GENERATED front door with 5 binding properties (generated / bounded / lossless-backed / count-asserted / spill-reachable) over the two-engine seam (PCB #44 horizontal + memory #35-43 vertical); spill-never-compress; the G1-G6 acceptance gate; the i59/i60+ sequence), `-front-door-redteam.md` (the confirmed breaks), `-front-door-hardened.md` (the AUTHORITATIVE s8 invariant set). The PCB renderer slim-ladder (96->72->56-char truncation, count-collapse, authority-trim-40, 20 KB section compression) is the named ANTI-PATTERN to remove at i59/i60+. **3 in-session cloud-subagent adversaries (D-0119; fronts: universality/judgment, spill/boundedness, currency/composition) CONFIRMED 7 breaks:** CB1 (ratification-blocking) the kernel is the wrong+insufficient recovery survivor (generator-dependent-by-construction yet asserted generator-independent; the read-only mount cannot delete=cannot rebuild; desktop-less regression); CB4 (ratification-blocking) currency does not compose across front doors (no compile epoch; per-commit re-ingest x N non-incremental producers = O(total knowledge) => C1 XOR G4; non-atomic close); CB2 the judgment carve-out swallows the universal law + re-imports F1/F2; CB3 "spill never compress" is retrieval-granularity + an integrity receipt, not a boundedness law (the B*/K*/one-descend pigeonhole); CB5 classifier-seam silent loss; CB6 synopsis nondeterminism breaks byte-identity; CB7 perpetual re-layer churn. Folded the AUTHORITATIVE hardened s8 (8 invariants, superseding the naive clauses): (1) recovery survivor set CLOSED = {kernel + a generator-independent cold-boot card} + a uniform rebuild interface; (2) honest boundedness = bytes flat, queries O(log_F N); (3) spill law restated + pin-the-unrecoverable + task-routable keys; (4) enumerable-or-frozen shell + computed hot-set (strike the hand-authored gotcha window) + a carve-out gate; (5) compile epoch + per-backing content-version currency + two-phase atomic close; (6) classifier oracle / honest G2; (7) synopsis = frozen replayed input; (8) batched provisioned re-layer. Acceptance gains G7 (synthesis-completeness) + G8 (cross-front-door consistency).
- **Reason:** START_HERE's cap event at the i57 close fired PB-7 (D-0152); a generated front door that SPILLS is the PB-7 answer already proven on the decision surface (PB-6, D-0151), so the cap event is the signal to generalize it. Freeze-then-red-team is the design-first discipline (the PB-7/D-0149 precedent); the red-team's job was to break the GENERALIZATION -- the two-engine architecture + the enumerable-interior re-layer HELD, the naive universal-boundedness claim did not, so the contract freezes WITH the hardened s8 authoritative. D-0119 permits in-session cloud subagents as the independent-grader boundary.
- **Alternatives:** freeze the naive contract as-is (REJECTED -- CB1/CB4 are ratification-blocking; the naive kernel would build the wrong i59); dispatch a fresh drafting worker (REJECTED -- the red-team subagents supply the independent boundary; inline Opus is the cached-context path); a frontier courier pack (REJECTED -- unneeded for a design freeze; the 3-adversary in-session pass is the established gate).
- **Consequence:** the universal front-door contract is FROZEN (hardened s8 authoritative). **i59 (root migration) is AMENDED by s8 rule 1: START_HERE -> the ~0.5-1 KB kernel AND a generator-independent cold-boot card (the kernel alone is insufficient) + register the uniform rebuild interface.** i60+ = class-by-class (CURRENT_STATE interior, handoff ledger, catalogs, backlogs, gotchas, tests, research), each design-first -> red-team-gated, carrying the s8 build requirements (pin-the-unrecoverable, task-match keys, classifier oracle, frozen-replayed synopsis, compile epoch, two-phase close). The DOC_PROTOCOL s2 front-door amendment is DEFERRED to i59 (operative at migration; the D-0141 re-layer-trigger rule already stands). AUDIT review_due i58 DISCHARGED (cadence check; no tier-prereq flip since i45; no spare audit lane -> review_due->i60; current_tier + next_increment unchanged; the w08 window-close defect rides any w08 touch, D-0134). NON-DISPLACING; P0-1 activation FROZEN (retrieved memory is EVIDENCE); SEALED_CHECK_47 retained (SP3/M-03 open); warm-pool/generators/video.interpret FROZEN (D-0080).
- **Affects:** NEW `research/2026-08-15-i58-universal-front-door-contract.md` + `-front-door-redteam.md` + `-front-door-hardened.md`; CURRENT_STATE (Next expected action + Last updated); FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF (s0/s3/s4/s11 rewrite; outgoing snapshot archived); PROCESS_BACKLOG (PB-7 row); AUDIT_PIPELINE (cadence header last_reviewed i58 / review_due i60); DECISION_LOG_INDEX (row); the PCB overlay frontier + BOOT_PACKET (N7 close-refold); archive/handoffs + ARCHIVE_INDEX; the doc-health/retrieval monitors; the GitHub mirror. No module code shipped (design/freeze wave).
- **State:** locked.

## D-0154 (2026-08-15) -- i59 CLOSED: the ROOT front-door migration -- START_HERE boot kernel + generator-independent cold-boot card + uniform rebuild interface (3-adversary red-teamed; architecture held, 13 gaps folded)

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Decision:** i59 executed the ROOT migration (D-0152, AMENDED by D-0153 s8 rule 1), the first increment of the universal derived-front-door program. `START_HERE.md` is reduced to a stable ~1 KB hand-maintained boot **KERNEL** that only ROUTES (Mode A: verify the PCB -> open the generated BOOT_PACKET; rebuild-ONCE via the uniform verb, then re-open; Mode B: read the cold-boot card; mount/mobile/Project-mirror take Mode B unconditionally) and holds NO changing state -- the one hand-maintained exception so a broken generator still leaves a trustworthy entrypoint. NEW `core-docs/COLD_BOOT_CARD.md` = the generator-INDEPENDENT recovery survivor; `{kernel + card}` is the CLOSED recovery set (the kernel alone is insufficient -- CB1). The card is near-frozen fixed-slot: as_of (close-id, not a not-yet-existing SHA), identity, source-of-truth rule, **7 PINNED never-spill constraints carried inline** (P0-1 activation-prohibited, never `git add -A`, lease order gpu->git->doc + <=1 GPU/wave, no orchestrator-driven external AI, UI live-GUI confirm, orphan discipline, D-0080 FROZEN set), a **count-asserted 23-doc canonical list** (N <= K_max=30), active-work as a **FROZEN REDIRECT** (not per-close synthesis), last-good close SHA, and a generator-free raw read order. NEW `ops/frontdoor/` = the **uniform rebuild interface**: `registry.json` (one class `root-pcb` at i59) + `Rebuild-FrontDoors.ps1` (the ONE O(1) verb the kernel names; non-vacuity floor) + `frontdoor-gate.py` (fail-closed close-step) + the **migration gate** (a class migrates only after registering a conformant {rebuild,verify} entry that rebuilds green). `DOC_PROTOCOL.md` s2 amended: START_HERE 6->2 KB, COLD_BOOT_CARD 5 KB, DOC_PROTOCOL 12->14 KB, an `ops/frontdoor/**` row, the front-door-law paragraph, and the mirror map (+ card + `claude/MIRROR_MANIFEST.md`). Design-first -> a 3-adversary in-session cloud-subagent red-team (D-0119; fronts recovery-survivability / boundedness-anti-churn / currency-composition): the `{kernel + card + uniform-rebuild}` ARCHITECTURE HELD; 13 confirmed gaps (5 ratification-blocking) were folded, notably the rebuild-once -> Mode-B non-looping edge (A1-B1), the pinned never-spill survivor slot (A1-B2), slot-5 free-form -> frozen redirect (A2-BREAK2), the doc-list K_max bound (A2-BREAK1), and -- most important -- the FALSE "atomic two-commit close" REPLACED by fail-closed-BY-DETECTION (A3-B1): the doc set commits first (generator-independent), the N7 close-refold's map/+generated/ is the Phase-2 epoch anchor, and Mode A refuses a packet whose epoch is behind the card (falling to Mode B), so a half-close is DETECTED not served. Driver proven GREEN on-box (root-pcb 0-stale, 18033 B packet). Frozen set: `research/2026-08-15-i59-root-migration-hardened.md` + `-redteam.md`. No module code.
- **Reason:** START_HERE's cap event fired PB-7 (D-0152); the hardened s8 (D-0153) established the kernel alone is insufficient (CB1) and mandated the generator-independent survivor + a mechanized O(1) rebuild bound. i59 is the first migration increment; design-first -> red-team-gated is the audit-pipeline discipline; D-0119 permits in-session cloud subagents as the independent-grader boundary; Nicholas selected the full hardened build + close.
- **Alternatives:** kernel-only survivor (REJECTED -- CB1); ship the naive pre-red-team design (REJECTED -- 5 ratification-blocking gaps); a claimed-atomic two-commit close (REJECTED -- contradicts the harvest-at-HEAD N7 ordering; A3-B1 -> fail-closed-by-detection instead); defer the frontdoor-gate + mirror-manifest to i60 (REJECTED -- Nicholas chose the full hardened build).
- **Consequence:** the ROOT is a fully-owned generated-front-door boot surface; `{kernel + card}` is the recovery survivor set; the uniform rebuild interface + migration gate are LIVE for i60+. i60+ = class-by-class (CURRENT_STATE interior, handoff ledger, catalogs, backlogs, gotchas, tests, research), each design-first -> red-team-gated + passing the `frontdoor-gate.py` migration gate, carrying the s8 build requirements (pin-the-unrecoverable, task-match keys, classifier oracle, frozen-replayed synopsis, compile epoch, two-phase close); the renderer slim-ladder is removed during migration. AUDIT review_due i60 NON-DISPLACING. P0-1 activation FROZEN (retrieved memory is EVIDENCE); SEALED_CHECK_47 retained (SP3/M-03 open); warm-pool/generators/`video.interpret` FROZEN (D-0080).
- **Affects:** NEW `core-docs/COLD_BOOT_CARD.md`; REWROTE `core-docs/START_HERE.md` (-> kernel); NEW `ops/frontdoor/{registry.json,Rebuild-FrontDoors.ps1,frontdoor-gate.py}`; NEW `research/2026-08-15-i59-root-migration-{hardened,redteam}.md`; `DOC_PROTOCOL.md` (s2 budgets + front-door law + mirror map); `CURRENT_STATE.md` (frontier + tests row + last-updated + slims); `FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF.md` (TL;DR/ledger/frontier/box); `PROCESS_BACKLOG.md` PB-7; `DECISION_LOG_INDEX.md` row; the map claim (`doc:core-docs/COLD_BOOT_CARD.md` entity + `START_HERE` one_line) + overlay frontier + the N7 close-refold; `archive/doc-snapshots/2026-08-15/START_HERE.md` + `archive/handoffs/2026-08-15-FANOUT_ORCHESTRATOR_HANDOFF-i59.md` + `ARCHIVE_INDEX.md`; `claude/MIRROR_MANIFEST.md`; both monitors; the GitHub mirror.
- **State:** locked.

## D-0155 (2026-08-15) -- i59 REOPENED for a planning-reconciliation addendum: control-plane truth reconciled + i60-i79 rolling roadmap; first PB-7 class migration DEFERRED i60->i68; Frontier Agent in the Deterministic Loop

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Decision:** i59 was reopened ONLY for planning + housekeeping reconciliation (Nicholas's directive; NO core logic changed; iteration stays 59, next 60). Reconciled stale/contradictory control-plane state and set a SINGLE i60 objective across all live planning surfaces. Rulings: (1) the project stays committed to PB-7 / index-derived bounded front doors, but the FIRST additional document-class migration is DEFERRED from i60 to i68 -- until the bootstrap is OBSERVABLY used bounded (F-i53-eff satisfied) and close orchestration is consolidated; (2) agent-facing ingest converges on task-selected GENERATED projections backed by canonical facts, not ever-growing hand-curated entry docs; (3) routine close adopts **Frontier Agent in the Deterministic Loop** -- Frontier judgment handles semantic impact / bounded correction / ambiguous dependencies, deterministic machinery handles declared updates / generation / validation / git+mirror reconciliation / restartability; routine close is NOT human-gated; (4) a bounded management/audit projection for Nicholas is observability + steering, NOT a per-close approval gate; (5) the i60-i79 roadmap is ROLLING (i60-i61 committed; later blocks = sequenced intent, revalidated at boundaries). **i60 = make the small handoff actually small, truthful, easy to use:** prove + ENFORCE bounded bootstrap/retrieval on fresh work, close F-i53-eff, repair FO-6, produce a bounded manager view, treat SP3/M-03 + stale version/test claims as explicit TRUTH questions (no silent activation). Blocks: i60-i61 bounded-ingest + control-plane; i62-i67 the one resumable close transaction; i68-i73 semantic-family front doors (current-work / health / system-catalog); i74-i76 memory-in-the-loop; i77-i79 inspectability + re-plan. Full plan: `research/2026-08-15-i59-roadmap-reconciliation.md`.
- **Reason:** i59 built the front-door machinery + migrated the root, but the small bootstrap is not yet CONSISTENTLY used as one (zero bounded opens observed i55/i58/i59), control-plane facts were unreconciled, and close remains scattered. ## D-0157 -- i60 follow-on: the retrieval --gate WIRED fail-closed into the close path

2026-08-16 (refines the D-0156 residual). The gen-retrieval-monitor `--gate` + `--artifacts-dir` cross-check (shipped i60 Lane 1, 88f41dee) is now WIRED into the canonical every-close driver `ops/close-refold.ps1`: new optional `-Ledger`/`-Iteration`/`-ArtifactsDirs`/`-GateOutDir` params; in fold mode, after render + render --check, the driver runs `gen-retrieval-monitor.py --ledger <L> --iteration <N> --gate --artifacts-dir ...`, emits the standing retrieval-bytes-log row, and THROWS (aborts the close) on a non-zero gate. Backward-compatible: skipped when -Ledger is not provided. The gate ENFORCES the ZERO-BOUNDED FLOOR (a session that whole-opened docs with zero bounded queries cannot close). i61 RAISES it to a meaningful bounded fraction + resolves the remaining residuals (some ledger properties self-reported; artifact reconciliation query-string based). This CORRECTS the D-0156 residual that described enforcement as "deferred i61 / observe-only": enforcement at the zero-floor is ACTIVE from i60. The i60 close itself passes the wired gate (bounded_fraction 0.027 > 0). AST-verified; the wiring is proven by running close-refold fold -Ledger during this correction. P0-1 stays FROZEN; F-i53-eff stays OPEN (adoption proof = i61). Record: research/2026-08-16-i60-bounded-ingest-design.md.
